**I. General Overview**
• **Full Name:** Sardar Vallabhbhai Patel Education Society Managed, R. N. G. Patel Institute of Technology – RNGPIT.
• **Established:** 2010 as a "Center for Excellence".
• **Affiliation:** Recognized by **AICTE**, New Delhi, and affiliated with **Gujarat Technological University (GTU)**, Ahmedabad.
• **Location:** Isroli-Afwa, Bardoli-Navsari Road, District Surat, Gujarat - 394620.
• **Accreditation:** The institute is accredited with an **'A+' grade by NAAC**.
**II. Academic Programs & Accreditations**
The institute offers several Bachelor of Engineering (B.E./B.Tech) programs, many of which are **NBA Accredited** (valid until June 2025).
**Accredited UG ProgramsDepartmentIntake CapacityComputer Science & Engineering**120–180 seats**Chemical Engineering**60 seats**Civil Engineering**60 seats**Mechanical Engineering**60 seats**Electrical Engineering**60 seats
*The institute also offers B.Voc, MBA (Logistics & Supply Chain Management), and Integrated M.Sc IT programs.*
**III. Placement Statistics (A.Y. 2023-24)**
RNGPIT maintains a strong track record of securing employment for its graduates across all core engineering sectors.
• **Computer Science & Engineering:** 92.3%
• **Chemical Engineering:** 83.25%
• **Mechanical Engineering:** 83.1%
• **Civil Engineering:** 81.34%
• **Electrical Engineering:** 80.5%
• **Highest Package Recorded:** Recently reached up to **INR 15 LPA** (domestic) and significantly higher for international offers.
**IV. Institutional Key Performance Indicators**
• **Students Benefited:** 2,427+
• **Total Placements:** 1,709+
• **Industrial Collaborations:** 35 MoUs with various industries
• **Testing & Consultancy:** 48 active projects (including NABL-accredited environmental audits)
**V. Infrastructure & Facilities**
• **Laboratories:** State-of-the-art facilities for practical training, including a dedicated Computer Center with 450+ high-speed computers.
• **Library:** A 400-square-meter facility with extensive physical and e-resources.
• **Campus:** A 12.25-acre eco-friendly campus with modern classrooms, hostels, and sports grounds.
• **Support:** Entrepreneurial funding and support provided through **SSIP (Student Startup & Innovation Policy)**.

**R. N. G. Patel Institute of Technology (RNGPIT)**.

### **1. Institute Overview**

- **Established:** 2010
- **Location:** Bardoli-Navsari Road, Isroli, Surat, Gujarat.
- **Affiliation & Approval:** Affiliated with Gujarat Technological University (GTU) and approved by AICTE, New Delhi.
- **Key Distinctions:** The institute is recognized for its high academic standards, often ranking in the top 10 of state-level university rankings. It is heavily focused on industry-ready professional training.

### **2. Academic Programs & Accreditation**

The institute offers several NBA-accredited undergraduate (B.E./B.Tech) programs:

- **Chemical Engineering** (Accredited up to June 2025)
- **Civil Engineering** (Accredited up to June 2025)
- **Computer Science and Engineering** (Accredited up to June 2025)
- **Electrical Engineering** (Accredited up to June 2025)
- **Mechanical Engineering** (Accredited up to June 2025)
- **Other Programs:** Also offers B.Voc, MBA, and Integrated M.Sc (IT).

### **3. Leadership Messages**

- **From the President (Shree Babubhai Vanmalibhai Patel):** Emphasizes providing quality education to empower youth and contribute to the nation's growth. He highlights the institution's commitment to creating social welfare through technical excellence.
- **From the Director (Dr. Latesh Chaudhari):** Focuses on the institute's role as a "Center for Excellence." He stresses the importance of practical training, industry-institute interaction, and maintaining high standards in engineering research and development.

### **4. Placement Performance (2023-24)**

The institute maintains a strong placement record across all engineering branches:

- **Computer Science & Engineering:** 92.3%
- **Chemical Engineering:** 83.25%
- **Mechanical Engineering:** 83.1%
- **Civil Engineering:** 81.34%
- **Electrical Engineering:** 80.5%
- **Stats:** Over **1,709 total placements** recorded with **2,427+ total offers** made to students.

### **5. Top Recruiters**

The institute has partnerships with several blue-chip and multinational companies, including:

- **Reliance Industries**
- **Adani Group**
- **Larsen & Toubro (L&T)**
- **Tata Consultancy Services (TCS)**
- **AM/NS India**
- **Blue Star**
- **Siemens Energy**
- **Globant**
- **Macleods Pharmaceuticals**

### **6. Collaborative Strength & Infrastructure**

- **Industrial Collaborations:** Over **35+ active MoUs** and collaborations with various industries for training and research.
- **Consultancy:** Conducts **48+ Testing & Consultancy** projects, specifically in environmental audits and civil engineering testing.
- **Facilities:** The campus includes modern laboratories, a computer center with fiber connectivity, a digital library, an auditorium, and extensive sports facilities.

### **7. Contact Information**

- **Address:** Bardoli-Navsari Road, Isroli, Afwa, Bardoli, Surat - 394620.
- **Website:** [www.rngpit.ac.in](http://www.rngpit.ac.in/)
- **Email:** info@rngpit.ac.in

---

### **1. Admission & Eligibility (2025-26)**

The institute follows the standardized Gujarat state admission process (ACPC).

- **Entrance Exams:** Admission is primarily based on **GUJCET** (Gujarat Common Entrance Test) or **JEE Main** scores.
- **Eligibility:** Candidates must have passed 10+2 with Physics and Mathematics as compulsory subjects, along with Chemistry/Biotechnology/Biology/Technical Vocational subjects.
- **Cutoffs (2025 Trends):** * **Computer Science & Engineering:** Highly competitive; often closes within the top state ranks.
    - **Chemical/Mechanical Engineering:** Closing ranks for the general category typically range between **30,000 and 50,000** in GUJCET.

### **2. Fees & Scholarship Schemes**

RNGPIT is known for being one of the more accessible private institutes in South Gujarat.

- **Annual Tuition Fees:** Approximately **₹66,000 – ₹70,000** per year.
- **Government Scholarships:**
    - **MYSY (Mukhyamantri Yuva Swavalamban Yojana):** Provides up to 50% fee assistance for students with 80+ percentile in 10+2.
    - **Digital Gujarat Portal:** Full fee waivers for SC/ST/SEBC students based on government norms.
- **Institutional Aid:** The **Dr. R.N.G. Patel Memorial Foundation** and the Alumni Association provide specific merit-cum-means scholarships to underprivileged students.

### **3. Student Life & Technical Clubs**

Beyond academics, the college hosts several active student chapters and clubs:

- **Technical Clubs:** Includes the **Google Developer Student Club (GDSC)**, **Akatsuki Coding Club**, and hardware innovation groups for IoT and Robotics.
- **Innovation:** The campus houses a **Common Engineering Facilities Centre (CEFC)** where students work on prototype development and industry projects.
- **Annual Events:** * **"Esperanza":** The signature national-level technical symposium.
    - **Cultural Fest:** Includes "Antarnaad" for performing arts and drama.

### **4. Campus Infrastructure & Facilities**

- **Hostels:** On-campus hostel facilities are available for both boys and girls. SC/ST students often receive free or subsidized hostel accommodation.
- **Library:** A massive **400 sq. meter central library** featuring a digital section, book bank facility, and barcode-based management (SOUL 2.0).
- **Connectivity:** The campus is equipped with Wi-Fi (up to 300 Mbps), though student reviews note that connectivity is primarily optimized for lab areas.
- **Transport:** Dedicated bus services connect the campus to major points in **Surat, Navsari, and Bardoli**.

### **5. Notable Placement Updates**

While the average package is around **₹2.5 – ₹3.5 LPA**, top performers have secured international offers and high-value domestic packages:

- **Highest Package recorded:** Mention of domestic packages up to **₹24.5 LPA** (from specialized recruiters like BFY Belgium).
- **Industry Exposure:** The college is a "TCS Accredited Institute," meaning students get preferential access to TCS recruitment drives and curriculum support.

### **1. Message from the President**

**Speaker:** Shree Babubhai Vanmalibhai Patel

- **Foundation:** The institute is governed by the **Sardar Vallabhbhai Patel Education Society (SVPES)**, a public trust established in **1996** dedicated to serving society through high-quality education.
- **Legacy:** Under the leadership of Mr. R. S. Patel, the trust launched the region's first polytechnic, **N. G. Patel Polytechnic (NGPP)**, in 1997.
- **Mission:** To prepare thousands of "technocrats" for various industries by focusing on quality technical education.

### **2. Message from the Director**

**Speaker:** Dr. Latesh Chaudhari, Ph.D. (Chemical Engg.), M.Tech. (Gold Medalist)

- **Vision:** The institute operates under the motto **"Taking you to Tomorrow’s Technology"**.
- **Balanced Education:** RNGPIT is committed to providing excellence in technical education through both **theoretical** and **practical** training.
- **Qualified Faculty:** The institute places a heavy emphasis on academic expertise:
    - It currently employs **12 faculty members with Ph.D. degrees**.
    - **16 faculty members** are actively pursuing their **Doctoral research** in various engineering fields.
- **Infrastructure:** The message highlights the development of **state-of-the-art laboratories** to support hands-on learning.

Based on the information provided in the uploaded images and supplemented by current 2024-2025 details, here is a comprehensive summary of **R. N. G. Patel Institute of Technology (RNGPIT)**.

### **1. Core Profile & Leadership**

- **Established:** 2010 as a "Center for Excellence".
- **Location:** Bardoli-Navsari Road, Isroli-Afwa, Surat, Gujarat.
- **Affiliation:** Affiliated with **Gujarat Technological University (GTU)** and approved by **AICTE**, New Delhi.
- **Leadership:**
    - **President:** Shree Babubhai Vanmalibhai Patel, representing the Sardar Vallabhbhai Patel Education Society (SVPES), which was established in 1996.
    - **Director:** Dr. Latesh Chaudhari (Ph.D. in Chemical Engineering), under whose leadership the institute maintains 12 Ph.D. faculty members and 16 others pursuing doctorates.

### **2. Academic Programs & Intake (2025-26)**

The institute offers undergraduate, postgraduate, and vocational programs.

| **Program Type** | **Specialization** | **Yearly Fees (Approx.)** |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **B.Tech / B.E.** | Computer Science, Chemical, Mechanical, Civil, Electrical, IT | **₹66,000 – ₹70,000** |
| **MBA** | Logistics and Supply Chain Management | **₹64,000** |
| **B.Voc** | Software Development, Production Tech, Solar Energy, etc. | **₹45,000** |
| **Integrated** | M.Sc (IT) - 5 Years | **₹35,000 (1st Yr)** |

### **3. Admissions & Cutoff Trends**

- **Entrance Exams:** Admission for engineering is based on **GUJCET** or **JEE Main**.
- **2024 Closing Ranks (General Category):**
    - **Computer Science & Engineering:** 17,770
    - **Chemical Engineering:** 36,800
    - **Mechanical Engineering:** 38,598
    - **Electrical Engineering:** 39,370

### **4. Placement & Industrial Connect**

The institute maintains strong industry ties with **35+ Technical Collaborations** and **48+ Testing & Consultancy projects**.

- **Placement Rates (2023-24):** Computer Science (92.3%), Chemical (83.25%), and Mechanical (83.1%).
- **Stats:** Over **1,709 placements** and **2,427 total offers** recorded.
- **Top Recruiters:** Reliance Industries, Adani, L&T, TCS (Accredited Institute), AM/NS India, Blue Star, and Siemens Energy.

### **5. Infrastructure & Student Life**

- **Library:** A 400 sq. meter facility with barcode management (SOUL 2.0 software) and a dedicated Digital Library section.
- **Connectivity:** Campus-wide Wi-Fi with speeds up to **300 Mbps**.
- **Innovation:** Features a **Common Engineering Facilities Centre (CEFC)** for applied research and prototype development.
- **Transport:** Dedicated bus services covering Surat, Bardoli, and Navsari.
- **Hostel:** On-campus separate hostels for boys and girls with R.O. water and basic furniture (approx. 52 seats).

### **6. Institutional Achievements**

- Recognized as a **"Schedule I Environmental Auditor"** by the Gujarat Pollution Control Board.
- Awarded the **"Green Campus Award"** by the Surat City Productivity Council.
- Successfully received a grant of **₹15 Lakhs** from the Student Startup and Innovation Policy (SSIP).

 **Governing Body of R. N. G. Patel Institute of Technology (RNGPIT).**

## **Overview**

The Board of Governors (BOG) is the primary body responsible for the management, strategic vision, and regulatory compliance of the institution.

---

## **BOG Committee Members**

| **Sr. No.** | **Category** | **Name of Member** |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Chairman | Mr. B. N. Patel |
| 2 | Executive Member | Mr. M. N. Patel |
| 3 | Executive Member | Mr. P. B. Patel |
| 4 | Executive Member | Mr. D. M. Patel |
| 5 | Executive Member | Mr. H. D. Patel |
| 6 | Executive Member | Mr. R. N. Patel |
| 7 | Executive Member | Mr. T. A. Naik |
| 8 | Member Secretary | Dr. L. B. Chaudhari |
| 9 | State Government Ex-officio | Dr. Sachin Parikh |
| 10 | Industrialist | Mr. Amit Patel |
| 11 | Industrialist | Mr. Niraj Mihani |
| 12 | Faculty Member | Dr. K. R. Rathod |
| 13 | Faculty Member | Mr. V. C. Joshi |

---

## **Key Functions & Responsibilities**

The BOG handles the administrative, financial, and academic governance of the college. Major duties include:

- **Financial Oversight:** Approving budget estimates (recurring and non-recurring) and accepting audited statements.
- **Strategic Planning:** Formulating the vision, mission, and long-term strategy for the institute, including Quality Improvement Programs for staff.
- **Compliance:** Ensuring adherence to regulations set by the AICTE, State Government, and GTU (Gujarat Technological University).
- **Academic Development:** Approving new programs leading to degrees and monitoring the academic performance of students and staff.
- **HR & Administration:** Approving staff appointments, delegating financial powers to the Principal, and regulating discipline among teaching and non-teaching staff.

---

 **Board of Governors (BOG)** details into three functional pillars: **Strategic Leadership**, **Financial Oversight**, and **Regulatory Compliance**.

---

## **1. Strategic & Academic Leadership**

The BOG acts as the "brain" of the institute, setting the long-term direction.

- **Vision & Mission:** Defining the core purpose of the college and its individual departments.
- **Program Expansion:** Deciding on new degree programs and educational activities.
- **Performance Monitoring:** Reviewing the Principal’s reports on student academic performance and admission status.
- **Institutional Growth:** Ensuring the campus has the necessary facilities and technology to meet modern engineering standards.

## **2. Financial & Resource Management**

The BOG holds the "purse strings" and ensures fiscal responsibility.

- **Budgeting:** Approving the annual budget, including both recurring expenses (salaries, utilities) and non-recurring expenses (new equipment, buildings).
- **Audit & Accountability:** Scrutinizing and accepting the yearly audited financial statements.
- **Resource Generation:** Ensuring the institute has enough funding and resources for both current needs and future developments.

## **3. Administration & Human Resources**

The BOG ensures the college runs smoothly and fairly for all employees.

- **Staffing:** Approving the appointment of teaching staff and creating strategies for staff development (Workshops, Higher Studies, and Quality Improvement Programs).
- **Governance & Power:** Delegating administrative and financial authority to the Principal for day-to-day operations.
- **Discipline & Welfare:** Regulating staff conduct and ensuring fair service conditions regarding leaves, retirement, and provident funds.

## **4. Regulatory Compliance**

The BOG serves as the liaison between the college and government bodies.

- **Standards:** Ensuring the college meets the strict criteria set by **AICTE**, the **State Government**, and the **University**.
- **Fee Structure:** Adopting the official fee structures dictated by the Fee Regulatory Committee.
- **Legal Adherence:** Implementing all government acts and instructions related to technical education.

# RNGPIT Committees - Detailed Summary with Tables

## 1. Anti-Ragging Committee

**Purpose:** To completely eliminate ragging in all forms and ensure the physical and psychological safety of all students, especially freshers.

**What constitutes ragging:**

- Teasing, treating rudely, or handling with rudeness any fresher or student
- Any act causing annoyance, hardship, psychological harm, or fear
- Forcing students to do acts they wouldn't ordinarily do
- Creating shame, torment, or embarrassment
- Showing superiority or power over other students

# Science and Humanities Department - RNGPIT

## About the Department

The Department of Science and Humanities serves as the **backbone of Engineering Education** at RNGPIT, providing essential foundation training in basic sciences to support all engineering departments.

### Core Subjects Offered

The department covers fundamental subjects that form the foundation of engineering education:

| Subject Area | Focus |
| --- | --- |
| **Mathematics** | Theoretical and applied mathematical concepts |
| **Physics** | Fundamental physical principles and applications |
| **Chemistry** | Chemical sciences and their engineering applications |
| **English** | Communication and language skills |
| **Environmental Science** | Environmental awareness and sustainability |
| **Management** | Basic management principles |

### Department Approach

- Provides **theoretical and application-oriented knowledge** to prepare well-rounded engineers
- Focuses on both **co-curricular and extra-curricular activities**
- Staffed by qualified faculty members dedicated to student development
- Equips students with insights necessary to become up-to-date engineers

---

## Vision

> "To enhance the newly admitted students' potentialities and make their base firm for engineering and their bright future."
> 

The department aims to strengthen the foundational capabilities of first-year students, preparing them for advanced engineering studies and successful careers.

---

## Mission

> "Committed to prepare the future engineer, from various locales and of various merit for higher academics with sound ethical values."
> 

The department is dedicated to:

- Preparing future engineers from diverse backgrounds
- Accommodating students of varying merit levels
- Building strong academic foundations for higher studies
- Instilling sound ethical values in students

---

# Science and Humanities Department - Laboratory Facilities

## 1. Physics Laboratory

The Physics Laboratory is equipped with modern apparatus to conduct various experiments covering fundamental and advanced physics concepts relevant to engineering applications.

### List of Experiments and Equipment

| Sr. No. | Experiment/Equipment | Purpose/Application |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | **LED Characteristics Study** | Study the characteristics of Light Emitting Diode (LED) |
| 2 | **Zener Diode Characteristics** | Study the characteristics of Zener diode |
| 3 | **P-N Junction Diode Study** | Study the characteristics of P-N Junction Diode |
| 4 | **Solar Cell Analysis (Single)** | Study voltage and current characteristics of Solar cells |
| 5 | **Solar Cell Combinations** | Study voltage and current of Solar cells in series and parallel combinations |
| 6 | **Young's Modulus Experiment** | Determination of Young Modulus of Elasticity of material by bending method |
| 7 | **Ultrasonic Interferometer (Liquid A)** | Velocity measurement of ultrasonic wave in given liquid sample using ultrasonic interferometer |
| 8 | **Ultrasonic Interferometer (Liquid B)** | Measurement of velocity of ultrasonic wave using ultrasonic interferometer |
| 9 | **LASER Beam Divergence** | Determine the angular divergence of LASER beam |
| 10 | **LASER Wavelength Measurement** | Determine the wavelength of LASER using diffraction grating |
| 11 | **Fiber Optics Experiment** | Measurement of Numerical Aperture (NA) of the given Fiber optic cable |
| 12 | **Semiconductor Band Gap** | Determination of band gap of Semiconductor: Germanium Chip using Four Probe Method |
| 13 | **Moment of Inertia Experiment** | Determine the moment of inertia of a flywheel about its own axis of rotation |

### Subject Areas Covered

| Area | Experiments | Engineering Relevance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Electronics & Semiconductors** | LED, Zener Diode, P-N Junction, Band Gap | Basic electronic components, semiconductor physics |
| **Renewable Energy** | Solar Cells (single & combinations) | Solar energy applications, renewable energy systems |
| **Material Science** | Young's Modulus | Mechanical properties of materials, structural analysis |
| **Wave Physics** | Ultrasonic Interferometer | Wave propagation, material characterization |
| **Optics & Photonics** | LASER experiments, Fiber Optics | Modern optical communications, laser applications |
| **Mechanics** | Flywheel Moment of Inertia | Rotational dynamics, mechanical systems |

##LINK 12:1. mechanical-engineering  ( https://rngpit.ac.in/department/mechanical-engineering/)

ABOUT DEPARTMENT
The department has excellent industrial interaction and contributes to the industry through regular interaction and sharing of knowledge and ideas. Keep itself up to date with the latest developments and trends in the field and with a dedicated faculty of highly qualified and experienced members in all streams of mechanical engineering. The department has developed laboratories for Basic Mechanical Engineering, Fluid Mechanics, Metallurgy, Fluid Power Engineering, Mechanical Measurements & Metrology, Internal Combustion Engine, Refrigeration and Air Conditioning, Production Technology, Heat and Mass Transfer, Dynamics of Machine, CAD/CAM, Automobile engineering with well-equipped workshop.

Vision
To establish a holistic teaching-learning eco-system with a vision to generate quality Mechanical Engineering professionals to contribute for economic growth of the society and the nation.

Mission
To ensure effective and efficient teaching methods for sound fundamentals.
To impart technical skill through effective collaboration and specialized Training.
To use modern equipments, software and technology for effective learning.
To develop creative dynamic technocrats, entrepreneurs and leaders.

Programme Educational Objectives(PEO)
Potential to apply the basic and advanced knowledge of Mechanical Engineering along with inter-disciplinary subjects to build a successful career.
To inculcate proficiency skills/trainings among students to excel either in higher education, research field or mechanical industries.
Ability to identify, develop and engage in contemporary engineering practices with modern tools to solve engineering related issues.
To exhibit confidence, motivation & innovative ideas to complex problems to become an entrepreneur/leader.

Programme Specific Outcomes (PSO)
Graduates will be able to solve real life problems by using computer aided engineering (CAE) tools of design, analysis, manufacturing and assembly of products.
Graduates will be able to apply their knowledge and skills of manufacturing, design, thermal and fluids for the industrial growth

MOU Signed
The department has also updated the laboratory as per requirement. For the overall development of students, the department conducts regular basis expert talks, industrial visits, and seminars/STTP/LTTP/Workshop of relevant subjects. As part of its advancement, the department has signed an MOU with PTC University. USA and developed “Authorized Training Centre (ATC) for CREO 3.0”. Till date, nearly 170 students are certified as CREO for Design Engineer by the ATC. Solidworks authorized training center established in the department in association with Engineering Technics, Vadodara, and Spaon Institute, Surat. Till date, nearly 300 students  are certified by Dassault System, France.

**ABOUT DEPARTMENT**

The Computer Science and Engineering Department has been established with an aim to prepare technocrats in the field of Computer, Software and Data Science. The department has developed a Computer Centre with 450 Nos. of computers connected in LAN with 35 Mbps internet connectivity and latest licensed applications & system softwares. To emphasize more on practical aspects with current trends and technologies, the department has developed various laboratories like Open Source Technology Lab, UNIX Lab, Network & Security Lab, Programming Lab and Project Lab including R&D Centre.

### **Vision**

To create a holistic teaching -learning ecosystem with a vision to prepare globally competent computer professionals for the development of society and the nation.

### **Mission**

- To ensure effective and efficient teaching learning process for sound fundamentals.
- To impart technical skill through effective hands on practice on advanced technologies and Value education.
- To prepare industry ready professionals by industry partnerships and entrepreneurs activities.
- To inculcate human values, research capabilities, leadership abilities and ethics in young minds.

### **Programme Educational Objectives (PEO)**

- The graduates will be able to interact with their peers in industries and society as an engineering professionals, entrepreneurs and leaders to set up technical ambience in the society
- The graduates will be able to reveal professionalism, ethical attitude, strong communication skills and maintain good team work spirit in their profession.
- The graduate will be able to utilize their skills with strong foundation to prepare them for higher learning and research activity.
- The graduate will be able to build up an ability to analyse the requirements, comprehend professional specifications, design and provide engineering solutions.

### **Programme Specific Outcomes (PSO)**

- Analyse, design and develop algorithmic solutions to any real life problems.
- To provide solutions for complex engineering problems by understanding the computer science concepts.
- To demonstrate the use of knowledge and ability to write programs and integrate them with software/hardware products in the domain of database, networking, data analytics, mobile communication technologies and web technologies.

Computerized Numerical Control (CNC) – Skill Development Program:
The CNC Machining Program prepares students for a high-demand, entry-level position as a CNC machine operator or general machinist. CNC operators work in a variety of manufacturing environments and are trained to program, set-up, and operate a computer numerical control (CNC) machine, inspect parts, perform production runs and set-up jobs. Demand for CNC machine operators is continuously rising in manufacturing businesses.

 

The RNGPIT in collaboration with SETU foundation, Surat offers CNC training for students from various cadres like Degree engineering, Diploma Engineering, Bachelor of Vocation etc.

 

Course Objectives:

To understand the main features and working parts of CNC machines and accessories (2 axis machines to 5 axis machines including machining centers)

Understanding and minimizing the hazards associated with machining operations on CNC machines.

Understand the common terminologies used in CNC Programming like program number; part number and name; tool names; operation names; motion commands; tool change positions and commands; tool numbers and offset numbers; subprograms and canned cycles; tool nose radius compensation commands; spindle, feed rate and coolant commands etc.

Understand and effectively use the methods of checking quality of shaped components against required standards; impact of depth of cut on chatter, surface finish; range of materials used in common engineering applications and identify materials by their physical properties. 

Read and establish job requirements of raw material / component, dimensions, limits and tolerances, surface finish, shapes etc.

Report and rectify inconsistent information, conduct prelim checks on machines, extract and use information from engineering drawings, identify tools and suitable work holding devices. 

Carry out programming for CNC Machine: ensure that the program is efficient with commands for tool motions, spindle motions etc. in syntax corresponding to the machine and control system of the CNC machine.

Transmit and check the errors; handle the typical problems with the programming, loading and editing activities effectively.

Course Content:

Basics of CNC machines

History CNC machines

Basic components of CNC machine like Chuck assembly, Drives, Turret, Tailstock, Slides etc.

Types of CNC Machines

Types of Control panels for CNC

Tool Selection for various operations performed on machines.

Work zero setup

Coordinate Geometry

Basic fundamentals of CNC programming for Turning and Milling.

Practice for Programming for Turning and Milling (manual).

Practice for Programming for Turning and Milling (on CNC simulator)

Hands on practice on machine

Basic understanding of CNC Machine Maintenance.

Facility at R. N. G. Patel Institute of Technology for the training related to CNC practice

SolidWorks is one of the products of SolidWorks Corporation, which is powered by Dassault Systemes 3D experience platform. SolidWorks mechanical design automation software is a feature-based, parametric solid modeling design tool that lets designers quickly sketch out ideas, experiment copy paste functionality same as in the Windows. Many of the icons such as print, open, cut and save are also part of the SolidWorks application which is familiar with Windows functions. When designing a model using SolidWorks, you can visualize it in three dimensions, the way the model exists with features and dimensions, and produce models and detailed drawings. It takes advantage of the easy to learn Windows graphical user interface in which designers can use simple drag and drop (DD), once it is manufactured.

In association with Engineering Technique (Vadodara- Makarpura GIDC), RNGPIT has developed Authorised Training Center (ATC) for the SOLIDWORKS software to impart design , manufacturing and production skill of students. Students can actually visulaise theire design or concept by making virtual 3D models using this licensed solidworks software at CAD Lab (D-101/102). Solidworks is widely used by many nearby manufacturing industries like DOMS industry, HLE etc .

Course Objective
The course aims to give students and professionals the essentials that are needed to become a certified SOLIDWORKS associate. The course will help individuals use the software with confidence and design/draft the next innovative thing. The course is designed to be a process or task-based approach to learning the individual features and functions of SOLIDWORKS, thereby emphasizing processes and procedures for completion of any task. By building actual parts and assemblies, the student should learn the necessary commands, options, and menus in the context of completing a design task.
Demonstrate competency with multiple drawing and modification commands in SOLIDWORKS.
Create three-dimensional solid models
Create three-dimensional assemblies incorporating multiple solid models.
Apply industry standards in the preparation of technical mechanical drawings.
Create Simulation of the assemblies incorporating multiple solid models.

3D Printing-Skill Development Program :
3D printing, also known as additive manufacturing, is a revolutionary process that allows the creation of three-dimensional objects from a digital file. Unlike traditional subtractive manufacturing methods, where material is cut away from a solid block, 3D printing builds objects layer by layer, adding material only where needed. Here’s a breakdown of the process:

Design Phase: The process begins with creating a 3D model of the desired object using computer-aided design (CAD) software. This digital model serves as the blueprint for the physical object.

Slicing: The 3D model is sliced into thin horizontal layers using slicing software. Each layer’s dimensions and geometry are calculated based on the specifications of the printer and the chosen material.

Printing: The sliced layers are sent to the 3D printer, which interprets the instructions and begins the printing process. Depending on the technology used, the printer deposits material layer by layer, fusing them together to form the final object. Common materials include plastics, metals, ceramics, and even food-grade substances.

Post-Processing: Once printing is complete, the object may undergo additional post-processing steps to improve its appearance or functionality. This could involve removing support structures, sanding rough edges, or applying surface finishes like painting or polishing.

3D printing offers numerous advantages over traditional manufacturing methods:

Complex Geometries: It enables the creation of intricate and complex designs that would be difficult or impossible to achieve with traditional manufacturing techniques.

Customization: Each object can be customized easily, allowing for personalized products tailored to individual needs or preferences.

Rapid Prototyping: 3D printing accelerates the product development cycle by allowing rapid prototyping and iterative design changes without the need for expensive tooling.

Resource Efficiency: It often results in less material waste compared to subtractive manufacturing, as only the necessary material is used in the fabrication process.

While 3D printing has seen significant advancements in recent years, including improvements in speed, resolution, and material options, it still has some limitations, such as material strength and surface finish. However, ongoing research and development efforts continue to push the boundaries of what is possible with this transformative technology.

 Objective: 

The 3D printing laboratory can serve as a dynamic hub for education, research, innovation, and community engagement, driving positive change and advancing the field of additive manufacturing. The following objectives could be fulfilled:

Education and Training

Research and Development

Innovation and Entrepreneurship

Community Engagement

Sustainability and Social Responsibility

Metal 3D Printer (SLM) : Selective Laser Melting

 

SLM printers utilize a high-powered laser to selectively melt and fuse metal powder particles layer by layer, resulting in fully dense metal parts with excellent mechanical properties.

SLM technology is widely used in aerospace, automotive, and medical industries for producing complex metal components with intricate geometries and superior strength.

Our institute’s SLM printers are equipped with advanced laser systems, inert gas chambers, and powder handling systems to ensure precise control over the printing process and high-quality metal parts.

Fused Deposition Modeling :

FDM printers utilize thermoplastic filaments, which are melted and extruded through a heated nozzle layer by layer to create objects.

These printers are versatile, affordable, and well-suited for producing prototypes, functional parts, and educational models.

Our institute offers a range of FDM printers with different build volumes and features to accommodate diverse project requirements.

LABORATORY DETAILS:

All the four NDT courses will be organized and conducted seamlessly without break for the convenience of participants from all over India and abroad.

Practical training on latest state of art equipments and eminent trainers from the institute as well as from industry.

The modern training centre is equipped with audio, visual power point presentations and modern training aids and fully established NDT & Material testing laboratory.

All the instruments are latest and directly used to inspect parts defect in industry.

 

 

 

 

Dye Penetrant Testing (DPT): 

Used to locate surface-breaking defects in all non porous materials like metals, plastics and ceramics. 

 

Magnetic Particle Testing (MPT): 

Process for detecting surface and slightly subsurface discontinuities in ferromagnetic materials such as iron, nickel, cobalt and some of their alloys. 

 

Ultrasonic Testing (UT):

Uses high frequency sound energy to conduct examinations and make measurements.

Used for flaw detection/ evaluation, dimensional measurements, material characterization and more. 

 

Radiographic Testing (RT):

Used extensively on castings and weldment. Radiography is well suited to the testing of semiconductor devices for cracks, broken wires, unsoldered connections, foreign material and misplaced components.

“An ounce of practice is better than tons of theory”. As then saying, the institute is equipped with the heaven of engineering with the state-of-art facilities that impart practical knowledge on machining, fitting, carpentry, welding, smithy, tin smithy, plumbing and foundry. It is equipped with all gear lathes, conventional lathes as well as wood working lathes. Machine shop is also equipped with Shaper, surface grinding and radial drilling machine, universal milling machines, EDM, CNC Turning center etc. A hand on practice is given to the students on all these machines. The airy, well-lit and well-ventilated workshop provides a perfect atmosphere for job work. Till date workshop has trained 120 students by various training programs for neighbor training institutes.

B.E. Mechanical -Working Professionals:
The program of B.E. Mechanical for Working Professionals is designed looking to the education needs in accordance with the job domain requirements. 
To apply for B.E. Working Professional, one must have passed a Diploma in the relevant specializations branch (Mechanical) in which the student wants to pursue B.E. 
The Duration of Working Professional for B.E Mechanical is 3 years as it is pursued by the Diploma Holders in the relevant specializations. 
B.E. degree for Working Professionals is approved by AICTE. 
The course contains of B.E. Working Professionals is designed according to the latest advancements in the engineering field.
 
Duration of B.E. Course for Working Professionals:

The B.E. for working professionals takes three years to complete the whole curriculum, which includes six semesters. Each semester lasts six months and includes a variety of courses ranging from the fundamentals to advanced levels of the technical curriculum. 
Because the students are coming from a Diploma program, they will be admitted immediately into the second year or third semester. Working professionals are awarded an extra year advantage in their career because their B.E. is just three years long.
Eligibility for Admission for B.E. for Working Professionals:

Professionals working in Registered Industry / Organization (Central / State) / Private/ Public Limited Company/ MSMEs located within 50 KM radial distance from the Institute.
Minimum of ONE Year Full time / Regular working Experience.
Qualification Same as candidates eligible for Lateral Entry to Second Year (As Specified in Approval Process Handbook published by AICTE from time to time)
Duration of the Course for Under Graduate Level:

Same as Lateral Entry to Second Year with a total course duration NOT less than THREE years and shall fulfil the total contact hours as prescribed by the Affiliating University for completion of duration of the course. 

(OR)

 

Same as Lateral Entry to Second Year with at least minimum of one semester extra to fulfil the total contact hours as prescribed by the Affiliating University for completion of duration of the course.

Academic Requirements:

Total Credits as specified in the AICTE Model Curriculum (or) Affiliating University are required to be earned for getting the Degree.
Syllabus shall be same as applicable to regular students.As per the AICTE Regulation up to 40% of credit transfer is permitted through MOOCs/SWAYAM. However for Diploma level institutes, other MOOCs platform similar to NPTEL/SWAYAM recognised by State/Central Government may also be considered.
Examination shall be same as the regular students.
Mode of Admission or Admission Policy:

As per the University /State Admission Policy. In absence of any such Admission Policy, Institute can devise transparent mechanism for short listing the eligible candidates in a highly transparent manner based on Merit of the Qualifying Examination.
Reservation Policy of the respective State/UT is applicable. However, One Seat in Every Institute shall be reserved for Candidates working in Central Government Organizations /Industries /Companies / Autonomous Organizations purely on MERIT Basis. In absence of any such Candidates the vacant seat may be transferred to General Category. 
Note: If any seats are vacant after the last round, candidates from other States/UTs may also be considered for admission, if they reside within a 50 KM radial distance from the institute.
State Government, Board & University Requirements:

State Government, DTE (Board) & University (as applicable) should devise a mechanism to affiliate the Working Professionals admitted in AICTE Approved Institutes for Courses under this Policy of AICTE.

RESULT OF 2020 analysis:
Key Observations
Highest Performance: The M 18 (5th sem) batch achieved the highest result with 68.33%.

Lowest Performance: There is a significant drop in the M 19 (3rd sem) batch, which recorded a result of only 15.85%.

Stable Range: The 7th and 5th-semester batches performed within a relatively similar range (mid-to-high 60s), while the 3rd-semester batch is a notable outlier.

RESULT OF 2017 analysis:
Data Analysis
Highest Performance: Semester 2 shows the highest result for both the College (74.23%) and GTU (69.13%).

Lowest Performance: Semester 3 recorded the lowest scores, with the GTU result dropping to 36.18%.

Trend: In every semester shown, the College Result is consistently higher than the GTU Result.

Largest Gap: The largest performance gap between the College and GTU occurred in Semester 5 (7.01% difference).


RESULT OF 2016 analysis:
Key Observations
Upward Trend: After a significant dip in Semester 3, both College and GTU results show a consistent upward trend through to Semester 7.

Peak Performance: The highest results for both categories were achieved in Semester 7, with the College reaching 84.50%.

Consistent Gap: In every single semester, the College Result outperformed the GTU Result.

Critical Point: Semester 3 represents the lowest point for this batch, with the College result dropping to 37.59% and the GTU result to 31.51%.


RESULT OF 2015 analysis:
Key Insights
Peak Performance: Both the College and GTU results reached their maximum in Semester 8, with the College achieving an impressive 98.56%.

Performance Dip: Similar to the other batches, Semester 3 shows a significant decline, particularly for the GTU results, which dropped to their lowest point of 30.42%.

Growing Gap: There is a notable disparity in performance during the middle semesters; for instance, in Semester 4, the College outperformed the GTU result by 26.46%.

Final Convergence: By the final year (Semesters 7 and 8), the gap between College and GTU results narrowed significantly as overall performance surged.

Welcome to the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at R. N. G. Patel Institute of Technology (RNGPIT).

The Department of Computer Science & Engineering under the RNGPIT is one of the core departments of the GTU and was established in 2010. Our department is proud to be NBA-accredited, reflecting our commitment to academic excellence and quality education. With a team of highly qualified and experienced faculty members, we provide a strong foundation in Computer Science and Engineering, ensuring our students receive the best learning experience.

We take pride in our academic excellence at Gujarat Technological University (GTU) and continuously strive to enhance our curriculum through industry collaborations, hands-on learning, and research-driven education. Our well-equipped laboratories support advanced learning, innovation, and project development.

Academic Programs
B.Tech (Honors) in Computer Science and Engineering with specipzations in Artificial Intelligence & Machine Learning (AI/ML), Data Science, Cyber Security, and Embedded System Development, with an intake capacity of 180 students.

B.Tech in Computer Science and Engineering for Working Professionals, providing an opportunity for diploma holders from all engineering branches to advance their careers.

Industry Collaboration & Career Support
Through various industrial collaborations and Centers of Excellence such as SAP, IBM, and Tata Strive, our department ensures 100% internship and placement support. These partnerships provide students with hands-on experience, real-world exposure, and career opportunities in leading technology companies.

Student Development & Research Support
To foster technical skills, leadership, confidence, and communication abilities, we have various student clubs that provide opportunities to engage in competitions, hackathons, and industry-led initiatives. For those passionate about research and entrepreneurship, our department offers strong support through SSIP grants, patent drafting assistance, article writing, and research paper guidance. We encourage students to explore innovation and contribute to technological advancements.

We invite students, researchers, and industry partners to collaborate with us in this journey of knowledge, innovation, and excellence. Let’s work together to shape the future of technology.

##LINK 14:3. information-technology  ([Information Technology – R N G Patel Institute of Technology](https://rngpit.ac.in/department/information-technology/))

The department of Information Technology (IT) was **established** in 2022 offering B.E. program in Information Technology with an intake of 60. Department also offers Hons. specialization in AI&ML. The department aims at imparting quality education in the realm of IT, software engineering and domain specific application with an objective to enhance and expand the knowledge and skill sets of target students, so that they can contribute in the betterment of Society at large. The main **objectives** of department are to provide Design and develop software products, Real time work experience and Employment. The department has well equipped **laboratories** like programming for problem solving using C / Python, data structures, database management system, programming with objects, Linux internals, big data and business analytics, case tools, web technologies, cloud application development, data warehousing and data mining, advanced python programming and object oriented modeling, etc. Department also has a **SAP Center of Excellence** and advance computing laboratories to cater advance technologies like AI, AR/VR, IoT, Data Science, Blockchain Technology etc.

### **Vision**

To empower IT graduates with cutting-edge knowledge and skills, enabling them to excel in a dynamic, technology-driven world, and contribute positively to society’s ever-evolving digital landscape.

### **Mission**

- To provide IT graduates with a world-class education that combines theoretical excellence with practical proficiency.
- To bridge the gap between academia and industry, facilitating internships, real-world projects, and networking opportunities for IT graduates
- To promote lifelong learning among IT graduates, enabling them to stay at the forefront of technological advancements throughout their careers.

### **Programme Educational Objectives(PEO)**

- The graduates will be able to interact with their peers in industries and society as an engineering professionals, entrepreneurs and leaders to set up technical ambience in the society
- The graduates will be able to reveal professionalism, ethical attitude, strong communication skills and maintain good team work spirit in their profession.
- The graduate will be able to utilize their skills with strong foundation to prepare them for higher learning and research activity.
- The graduate will be able to build up an ability to analyse the requirements, comprehend professional specifications, design and provide engineering solutions.

### **Programme Specific Outcomes(PSO)**

- Analyse, design and develop algorithmic solutions to any real life problems.
- To provide solutions for complex engineering problems by understanding the computer science concepts.
- To demonstrate the use of knowledge and ability to write programs and integrate them with software/hardware products in the domain of database, networking, data analytics, mobile communication technologies and web technologies.

### **MOU Signed**

- Department has signed M.O. U. with IBM Skillsbuild to provide 10000+ free IBM courses and free IBM Cloud Access and Training.
- Department has signed M.O. U. with SAP INDIA support for Excellence(COE) to give training on various SAP courses.
- Department has signed M.O. U. with Tata Strive SPOKE MODEL to give training in different various Emerging Technologies.
- Department has signed M.O. U. with LoftySoft AB (Sweden), Getwings Technologies Pvt. Ltd (Ahmedabad) for setting up “IoT Lab” and Centre of excellence in future.
- Department has signed M.O. U. with D-Link for D-Link Academy – An authorized Training Centre for D-Link certified Network Engineers.
- Department has been nominated as “Resource Centre” for Spoken Tutorial by IIT, Bombay, A project founded by MHRD, Government of Maharashtra.

##LINK 15:civil-engineering ([Civil Engineering Department – R N G Patel Institute of Technology](https://rngpit.ac.in/department/civil-engineering/))

### 🏗️ Civil Engineering Programs Offered

- **B.Tech. in Civil Engineering**
- **B.Tech. (Hons.) in Civil Engineering**
    - Specialization: Construction Technology
- **B.Voc. in Building Construction Technology**
- **B.Tech. Civil Engineering for Working Professionals (D2D)**
    - Direct admission into Second Year

### ✅ Accreditation

- **Approved by**: National Board of Accreditation (NBA)

### **ABOUT DEPARTMENT**

- Welcome to our Civil Engineering Department, where passion drives purpose! Established in 2010, we are more than educators; we are architects of dreams and builders of careers. Our mission is clear: to create strong connections with the construction industry, academia, and research fields, fostering a holistic approach to civil engineering education and real-world applications. At our core, we thrive on active engagement in fundamental and applied research, contributing significantly to our field’s knowledge base. Our experienced faculty, experts in areas like Concrete Technology and Structural Engineering, bridge the gap between theory and practice, ensuring our students are well-prepared for the dynamic world of civil engineering. We believe in learning by doing, organizing industrial visits, expert lectures, and seminars that immerse students in real-world situations and cutting-edge technology. Our students grasp practical industry demands, giving them a competitive edge. Since 2020, our “Civil Material Testing Laboratory (CMTL)” has been a symbol of quality assurance, accredited by NABL and approved by GERI-Vadodara. We ensure precise testing, serving government departments and private agencies with dedication and affordability. In summary, our department nurtures well-rounded civil engineers. With dedicated faculty guidance and a focus on research and practical applications, our students gain technical expertise, resilience, adaptability, and problem-solving skills essential for success in the evolving world of civil engineering. Join us, and let’s build a future where challenges are opportunities and dreams are foundations!

### **Vision**

To establish a holistic teaching-learning ecosystem with a vision to create competent civil engineers and entrepreneur for the development of society and the nation.

### **Mission**

- To achieve quality in education through creating collaborative environment.
- To stimulate students for effective and efficient learning.
- To create civil engineering professionals with ability to apply knowledge professionally to reach their highest potential.
- To develop creative, dynamic technocrats and entrepreneurs.

### **Programme Educational Objectives(PEO)**

**An Experienced Civil Engineer of program:**

- Shall be able to apply fundamental technical knowledge and skills to find creative solutions to technological challenges and problems in various areas of civil engineering.
- Shall be able to analyse, design and use skills in order to formulate and solve civil engineering problems.
- Shall be able to practice civil engineering in a responsible professional and ethical manner and implement eco-friendly sustainable technologies for the benefits of industries and society.
- Shall be able to engage in research and development in civil engineering and allied branches of it.

### **Programme Specific Outcomes(PSO)**

**An Experienced Civil Engineer of program:**

- Shall be competent in all civil-related execution work by offering sustainable solutions to civil engineering problems both industry and society face.
- Shall be able to analyse and use advance technology for better performance and quality control of civil projects by workmanship and material.

##LINK 16:1. electrical-engineering ([Electrical Engineering – R N G Patel Institute of Technology](https://rngpit.ac.in/department/electrical-engineering/))

### ⚡ Electrical Engineering Programs Offered

- **B.Tech. in Electrical Engineering**
- **B.Tech. (Hons.) in Electrical Engineering**
    - Specialization: Electric Vehicle
- **B.Tech. Electrical Engineering for Working Professionals (D2D)**
    - Direct admission into Second Year

### ✅ Accreditation

- **Approved by**: National Board of Accreditation (NBA)

**ABOUT DEPARTMENT**

“Education is not received, It is achieved” With this motto Electrical Engineering Department was established in year 2010 with the vision to convey practical knowledge in coordination with various Electrical industries and power sector companies. The department is persistently committed to enhancing technical knowledge along with the ethical values of the students to make them ready for the competitive world. With highly qualified & experienced faculties department has consistently tried to update itself for the constantly changing industrial needs, skills and challenges emerging out of new research also ensuring the best academic results from the students. The department is well equipped with 18 laboratories namely Elements of Electrical Engineering, Electrical & Electronics Workshop, Electrical Circuit Analysis, Electrical Machines (DC Machines & Transformer, AC Machines), Analog Electronics and Digital Electronics, Power System Protection, Control System Theory, Power Electronics, Electronic Measurement and Measuring Instruments, High Voltage Engineering, Elements of Electrical Design & Engineering Machine Design, Microprocessor and Microcontroller, Advanced Power Electronics, Advanced Power System, Interconnected Power System, Industrial Instrumentation, Computer Lab, Project Lab. All the laboratories are well equipped with the latest instruments & equipment which are updated on a regular basis which leads students to realize theory aspects intensely.

### **Vision**

To establish a holistic teaching-learning ecosystem with a vision to create competent Electrical technocrats with ethical values contributing to the sustainable growth of society and the nation.

### **Mission**

- **M1:** To ensure effective and efficient teaching methods with technical excellence for sound fundamentals.
- **M2:** To impart technical skills through effective practical work and Vocational Training.
- **M3:** To use modern tools and technology for better correlation of theory with practices.
- **M4:** To develop creative, dynamic technocrats and entrepreneurs.

### **Program Outcomes**

- **PO1 Engineering knowledge:** Apply the knowledge of mathematics, science, engineering fundamentals, and an engineering specialization to the solution of complex engineering problems.
- **PO2 Problem analysis:** Identify, formulate, research literature, and analyze complex engineering problems reaching substantiated conclusions using first principles of mathematics, natural sciences, and engineering sciences.
- **PO3 Design/development of solutions:** Design solutions for complex engineering problems and design system components or processes that meet the specified needs with appropriate consideration for public health and safety, and cultural, societal, and environmental considerations.
- **PO4 Conduct investigations of complex problems:** Use research-based knowledge and research methods including design of experiments, analysis and interpretation of data, and synthesis of the information to provide valid conclusions.
- **PO5 Modern tool usage:** Create, select, and apply appropriate techniques, resources, and modern engineering and IT tools including prediction and modelling to complex engineering activities with an understanding of the limitations.
- **PO6 The engineer and society:** Apply reasoning informed by the contextual knowledge to assess societal, health, safety, legal, and cultural issues and the consequent responsibilities relevant to the professional engineering practice.
- **PO7 Ethics**: Apply ethical principles and commit to professional ethics and responsibilities and norms of the engineering practice.
- **PO8 Individual and team work**: Function effectively as an individual, and as a member or leader in diverse teams, and in multidisciplinary settings.
- **PO9 Communication:** Communicate effectively on complex engineering activities with the engineering community and with society at large, such as being able to comprehend and write effective reports and design documentation, make effective presentations, and give and receive clear instructions.
- **PO10 Project management and finance:** Demonstrate knowledge and understanding of the engineering and management principles and apply these to one’s own work, as a member and leader in a team, to manage projects and in multidisciplinary environments.
- **PO11 Life-long learning:** Recognize the need for, and have the preparation and ability to engage in independent and life-long learning in the broadest context of technological change.

### **Program Specific Outcomes (PSO)**

Graduate Electrical Engineer will be able to

- **PSO1:** Identify, formulate and solve realistic electrical problems in operation of Electrical machines, and equipment used in power generation, transmission and utilization.
- **PSO2:** Proficient in assessing clean energy options, and energy-efficient solutions to promote sustainable development.

### **Program Educational Objectives(PEO)**

- **PEO1:** Graduates will apply basic and advanced knowledge to solve complex electrical problems and usage of modern tools in the field of electrical engineering along with inter-disciplinary fields.
- **PEO2:** Graduates will apply academic knowledge to formulate and address real-world phenomena contributing to innovative solutions, and upholding professional values.
- **PEO3:** Graduates will master skill-based knowledge to prepare them for higher education competitive exams and an entrepreneurial mind-set for successful career paths in electrical engineering.
- **PEO4:** Graduates will exhibit effective communication skills, teamwork and leadership qualities for society’s betterment and lifelong learning.

### **MOU Signed**

Who’s talking?
This is a message from the Chairman, Shree Babubhai Vanmalibhai Patel.

The Main Points:

History: The society started with a Polytechnic in 1997, and RNGPIT (the Degree College) was launched in 2010.
Quality Status: A huge chunk of their courses are NBA accredited. They are also the 2nd institute in India to get "5S-Workplace Management System" certification.
Cool Facilities & Training:
They have a Vocational Training Centre with 14 fancy labs.
SAP Centre of Excellence: They offer free SAP certified training to help students get jobs.
Wind Energy: They are a training partner for the "Vayumitra" skill program (sponsored by the Gov of India).
The Big Flex: They established India’s first Common Engineering Facility Centre (CEFC) on campus. It supports the textile, diamond, and auto industries with design and prototype centers.
Consultancy: They have government-approved labs for Environmental Audits and Civil Material Testing.
Student Life: Every year they host a state-level tech fest called "Visvesmruti".

Location:
The college is located on Bardoli – Navsari Road, District Surat, Gujarat.

Chemical Engineering:

**Course & Accreditation**

- **Degree:** Bachelor of Technology (B.Tech.) in Chemical Engineering.
- **Honors Option:** B.Tech. (Hons.) with specialization in **Industrial Process Safety**.
- **Status:** Approved by the **National Board of Accreditation (NBA)**.
- **Special Mode:** They have AICTE permission for a **Diploma-To-Degree (D2D)** mode specifically for **Working Professionals**.

**Department Profile**

- **Established:** 2010.
- **Key Achievement:** Recognized as a **Schedule-I Environmental Auditor** by the **Gujarat Pollution Control Board (GPCB)**. They actively conduct environmental audits for chemical industries.
- **Student Chapter:** They have an active **IIChE (Indian Institute of Chemical Engineers)** chapter for expert talks and industrial visits.

**Placements (2022-23 Highlights)**
The page showcases a placement banner with several recruiters and packages, including:

- **Companies:** Adani, Macleods, Sterlite Power, Aarti Drugs, Colourtex, Nitrex, etc.

- **Tech & Training:** M/s Indigenous Solution (CHEM CAD software), AMCAT, Quality Circle Forum India.

**Vision & Mission**

- **Goal:** To create technical, ethical, and socially responsible engineers.
- **Focus:** Practical work, vocational training, use of modern instruments, and entrepreneurship.

# integrated-mba-2

#MAIN (https://rngpit.ac.in/department/integrated-mba-2/) :
The Integrated MBA program at the R.N.G. Patel Institute of Technology (RNGPIT) in Bardoli is designed to streamlines the transition from foundational business studies to advanced management expertise.

Here is a summary of the department’s key highlightsKey highlights of the department:

**Program Overview**

**Dual Degree Pathway:** A five-year integrated program that combines aing BBA and MBA, saving students time and providing a competitive edge in the job market.

**Diverse Specializations:** Beyond the integrated track, the department offers specialized MBAs in Logistics & Supply Chain Management, as well aplus options for online learning and working professionals.

**Industry-Driven Curriculum: The c** Coursework focuses on core pillars such as leadership, finance, marketing, and entrepreneurship, blending academic theory with practical application.

**Key Benefits**

**Practical Experience:** Students engage in real-world projects, internships, and direct interactions with industry experts.

**Modern Infrastructure: Learning is supported by s** State-of-the-art facilities and asupport learning, with faculty led by Dr. Manish Pathak.

**Skill Development:** The program emphasizes critical thinking, ethical leadership, and a global business perspective to prepare students for managerial roles or entrepreneurial ventures.

#Industrial Visit
At R.N.G.P.I.T., we organize regular industry visits to bridge the gap between classroom learning and real-world applications. These visits provide students with valuable exposure to industrial environments, processes, and technologies. By interacting with professionals, students gain practical insights into current industry practices. Such experiences help them connect theoretical knowledge with practical implementation, preparing them for future careers.November 2017

# **M.B.A. (Logistics and Supply Chain Management)**

## **(Management)&Integrated M.B.A. (**https://rngpit.ac.in/department/mba-in-logistics-supply-chain-management/**)**

### **ABOUT DEPARTMENT**

The Department of Management at R. N. G. Patel Institute of Technology is dedicated to providing quality education in the field of management. Under the umbrella of Sardar Vallabhbhai Patel Education Society, Bardoli, the department aims to groom future leaders and entrepreneurs equipped with modern management skills and knowledge.

**PROGRAMS OFFERED**

- **Programme Name:** Master of Business Administration (M.B.A.) in Logistics and Supply Chain Management**Course Duration:** 2 Years**Admission Eligibility:** Any Graduate
- **Programme Name:** Master of Business Administration (M.B.A.) **(For Working Professionals )** in Logistics and Supply Chain Management **Course Duration:** 2 Years
- **Admission Eligibility:** Any Graduate(With 1 Year Work Experience , Working Place Should be within 75 Km of Area to the Institute)
- **Programme Name:** Integrated M.B.A. **Programme Duration:** 5 Years**Admission Eligibility:** 10 + 2 (Any Stream)

### **SPECIAL FEATURES**

- **Industry-Relevant Curriculum:** The department offers a comprehensive curriculum designed to meet the demands of the dynamic business environment, focusing on practical application and industry relevance.
- **Experienced Faculty:** Our faculty members are seasoned professionals with extensive industry experience and academic expertise, ensuring high-quality education and mentorship.
- **Industry Collaborations:** We foster strong partnerships with leading industry players to provide students with exposure to real-world challenges, internships, and placement opportunities.
- **State-of-the-Art Infrastructure:** The department is equipped with modern facilities, including smart classrooms, a dedicated library, computer labs, and seminar halls, providing an enriching learning environment.
- **Holistic Development:** Alongside academic rigor, the department emphasises holistic development through co-curricular activities, workshops, seminars, and guest lectures by industry experts.
- **Research and Innovation:** We encourage research and innovation through student projects, research publications, and participation in conferences, fostering a culture of continuous learning and intellectual growth.
- **Placement Assistance:** The department provides comprehensive placement assistance to students, facilitating internships, industry interactions, and campus recruitment drives with reputed companies.

The Department of Management at R. N. G. Patel Institute of Technology strives to nurture competent, ethical, and socially responsible management professionals who can make meaningful contributions to the global business landscape.

### **Vision**

To be the premier hub of ethical excellence in Management education, shaping dynamic leaders, technocrats, and researchers dedicated to driving industrial, social, and economic advancement on a national scale.

**Mission**

- To foster excellence and ethical growth by nurturing students competencies, skills, and attitudes for employability, entrepreneurship, and research prowess.
- To provide an infrastructure and environment that promotes academic freedom, values knowledge acquisition, and establishes our institution as the preferred choice for aspiring leaders.
- To cultivate and retain a cadre of competent, creative faculty members empowered to develop future leaders who contribute significantly to the nation.
- To forge strategic collaborations with industries, government bodies, and research institutes to cultivate innovative technocrats poised to address contemporary challenges.

### **Program Outcomes**

- Generic and Domain Knowledge – Ability to articulate, illustrate, analyze, synthesize and apply the knowledge of principles and frameworks of management and allied domains to the solutions of real-world complex business issues.
- Problem Solving & Innovation – Ability to Identify, formulate and provide innovative solution frameworks to real world complex business and social problems by systematically applying modern quantitative and qualitative problem solving tools and techniques.
- Critical Thinking – Ability to conduct investigation of multidimensional business problems using research based knowledge and research methods to arrive at data driven decisions.
- Effective Communication – Ability to effectively communicate in cross-cultural settings, in technology mediated environments, especially in the business context and with society at large.

### **Program Educational Objectives(PEO)**

- To equip students with a comprehensive understanding of management principles, theories, and practices, enabling them to effectively analyze and solve complex business challenges.
- To develop students’ leadership, communication, and interpersonal skills, fostering their ability to collaborate, communicate, and lead diverse teams in dynamic business environments.
- To instill a strong ethical foundation and a sense of social responsibility in students, empowering them to make ethical decisions and contribute positively to society.
- To cultivate a global mindset among students, preparing them to thrive in a multicultural and interconnected world, and to adapt to the rapid changes in the global business landscape.

### **Program Specific Outcomes(PSO)**

- Graduates will demonstrate proficiency in applying management concepts, frameworks, and analytical tools to identify, analyze, and solve complex business problems.
- Graduates will integrate ethical principles and social responsibility into their decision-making processes, demonstrating a commitment to ethical conduct and sustainability in business practices.
- Graduates will possess the ability to adapt to and thrive in diverse and dynamic business environments, demonstrating resilience, flexibility, and agility in responding to changes and challenges.
- Graduates will exhibit effective leadership, communication, and teamwork skills, enabling them to collaborate, negotiate, and lead diverse teams to achieve organizational goals.
- Graduates will demonstrate proficiency in utilizing technology and data analytics to inform decision-making and drive innovation in various functional areas of management.
- Graduates will exhibit strong entrepreneurial and managerial skills, demonstrating the ability to identify opportunities, develop business plans, and manage resources effectively to achieve organizational objectives.

## CAREER OPPORTUNITY (https://rngpit.ac.in/department/mba-in-logistics-supply-chain-management/placement/) :

### **Career Opportunities**

- Logistics and Supply Chain Management has become prominent and acknowledged as critical factor in establishing competitive advantage in several developed countries.
- Expansion of international trade and globalization strategy of many companies has increased importance of Logistics and Supply Chain Management. Aggregate cost of Logistics and Supply Chain can account for upto 30% of total landed cost of goods.
- Giving careful attention to Logistics and Supply Chain Management results in reduction in costs and thus better performance for companies Logistics and Supply Chain Management Industry directly or indirectly contributes estimated 25% to most countries GDP and employs several million people.

### **Logistics and Supply Chain Management Involve:**

- Effective day to day management of: Transport, Storage and Warehousing, Inventory/Stock Control, Purchasing, Packaging, Manufacturing Management
- Logistics and Supply Chain Management integrates with and is essential to many areas of business operations such as retailing, production management, quality control, importing and exporting, project management, forecasting etc.

### **Attracting Talent**

- Demand for qualified personnel in Logistics and Supply Chain Management far exceeds supply.
- Need to break perception that the sector is only “trucks and sheds”. The sector provides variety of career pathways with progression opportunities through to senior management.
- Workforce and working patterns keep changing and thus need to target demographics which are entering workforce in large numbers, such as women and youth.
- Today only approx 20% of Logistics and Supply Chain Management professionals are women.

### **Career Opportunities and Potential Employers**

- Companies today realize the critically important role that supply chain plays in the financial performance of the organization. Companies are looking for supply chain professionals that can transform their supply chain into a source of competitive advantage.
- In today’s global economy, companies like Dell, Nokia, Proctor & Gamble, Toyota, and Wal-Mart consider SCM to be a key factor in their success. SCM makes it possible to build and deliver products better, faster, and cheaper. Supply chain managers are the “glue” that connects the different parts of the organization.
- Businesses realize that being competitive in the 21st century requires leading edge thinking around supply chain management and logistics. Companies large and small are looking for talented individuals with a strong mix of education, potential, and motivation to manage and lead their global supply chains. You will find SCM career opportunities in a variety of organizations such as manufacturing and production companies, service providers, retailers, transportation companies, third party logistics firms (3PL), government agencies, and consulting firms. The array of companies needing supply chain expertise is nearly endless. Successful firms such as Disney, Hewlett-Packard, Boeing, FedEx, Nike, Nestle, and Best Buy are just a few examples of companies that actively hire SCM graduates.

### **Potential Employers for Supply Chain and logistics**

# Placement:

### **1. Institute & Department Overview**

- **Institute Profile:** RNGPIT is an Autonomous Institute (U/s UGC Act 1956) managed by the Sardar Vallabhbhai Patel Education Society. It is affiliated with **GTU** and approved by **AICTE**.
- **Department Establishment:** The Chemical Engineering Department was established in **2010**.
- **Vision/Mission:** Focuses on holistic teaching, practical work, vocational training, and developing creative, ethical technocrats.

### **2. Accreditations & Recognitions**

- **NBA Accredited:** The B.E. Chemical Engineering program is accredited by the National Board of Accreditation (Tier II) valid up to **30-06-2025**.
- **NAAC Accredited:** The institute holds an **A+ Grade**.
- **NABL Accredited Lab:** The Environmental Audit Consultancy Laboratory is accredited under **ISO/IEC 17025:2017** for "Testing" (Valid until **04/06/2025**).
- **GPCB Auditor:** Recognized as a **Schedule-I Environmental Auditor** by the Gujarat Pollution Control Board.
- **5S Certification:** The campus is **5S Certified** for workplace management.

### **3. Academic Programs Offered**

- **B.E. / B.Tech.** in Chemical Engineering.
- **B.E. (Hons.)** with specializations in:
    - **Industrial Process Safety**
    - **Waste Technology**.
- **Diploma-to-Degree (D2D) for Professionals:** A special mode for working professionals in registered industries (Central/State/Private/MSME) requiring a minimum of **one year of experience**.

### **4. Faculty Details**

The department is led by **Dr. Himanshu P. Kohli** (HOD & Associate Professor, Ph.D.). The team includes:

RICHE 2019GUJARAT COUNCIL OF SCIENCE & TECHNOLOGY(GUJCOST) & DEPARTMENT OF SCIENCE & TECHNOLOGY(DST), GOVERNMENT OF GUJART Sponsored 2 days first ever National conferences on “Recent innovations in Chemical Engineering(RICHE-2019), Organized by Chemical Engineering Department of R.N.G. Patel Institute of Technology. Total more than 80 Papers were presented by the faculties as well as students of different institutes in the conference, All the papers presented in conference are published by “Ria Publications” with International Standard Book Number(ISBN) in conference proceedings. Isroli. There were total 5 sections conducted in the conference first 3 on dated 18th Jan 2019 and remaining 4th and 5th on 19th Jan 2019. Each section there were best article award after screening of all the papers. Also different Keynote speakers were invited from the well known institutes were invited.

**SAP supported Center of Excellence**

**IBM Skillsbuild**

**TATA Strive Spoke Model**

Computer Science & Engineering department has established SAP sponsored “Center of Excellence(CoE) on Emerging Technologies” with Edunet Foundation. Industry experts are giving training on various SAP Course’s. After completion of course students are getting internship and placement opportunities.

Under this Centre of Excellence, CSE students will get opportunity to be familiar with SAP Open Learning Platform and get Certification in the courses that are most relevant for the Engineering students considering the current skill trends required in the job market.

**Courses Offered:**

- SAP certified citizen developer associate, getting started with UX, SAP business technology platform, SAP Analytics cloud, ABAP programming, Foundations of SAP Commerce Cloud and SAP S/4HANA Cloud Integration.
- Industry experts will provide training on industry relevant topics like IOT, Artificial Intelligence, Machine Learning, Deep Learning, Computer Vision, High Performance Computing and Capstone Projects.

**Objective/Outcome:**

- To impart practical skills of various SAP modules and get industry ready SAP certified professionals.
- To provide training and certification on emerging technologies from industry experts.
- To provide placement support to the certified students.

**Till date total 234 students of Computer Science & Engineering department are pursuing training and 78 students are already trained and certified.**

**Extracted Information – Short Summary**

**Learner Journey Stages**

1. **Onboarding of Learners** – Entry and orientation into the program
2. **Program Delivery** – Structured teaching and learning process
3. **Expert Mentoring** – Guidance from industry/subject experts
4. **Showcase Opportunities** – Platforms to present skills and projects
5. **Internship and Job Fairs** – Career exposure and placement opportunities
6. **Achievements & Recognition** – Outcomes symbolized by awards/trophies

Welcome to the Google Developer Student Club! GDSC is a community of like-minded students passionate about learning and exploring Google technologies. Our club provides a platform for students to enhance their technical skills, collaborate on projects, and connect with industry professionals. By joining a GDSC, students grow their knowledge in a peer-to-peer learning environment and build solutions for local businesses and their community. Join us for interactive workshops, tech talks, and events conducted by experts and professionals. Whether you’re a beginner or an experienced developer, GDSC is a welcoming community for everyone interested in technology.
