    Computer Science and Engineering department of R. N. G. P. I. T. institute has acquired institutional membership under CSI in 2020. Computer Society of INDIA (CSI) is the oldest and largest organisation of computer professionals in India. CSI facilitates research, knowledge sharing, learning and career enhancement among all categories of computer professionals. CSI promotes and provides financial support for research work in contemporary areas of computer Science. CSI provides resources for selecting project definition, for career guidance, allowing for downloading study material, publications, and participation in various competitions. One of the major initiatives of the CSI is to serve the student community by bridging the gap between academia and industry.

##LINK 14:1. information-technology  ([Information Technology – R N G Patel Institute of Technology](https://rngpit.ac.in/department/information-technology/))

The department of Information Technology (IT) was **established** in 2022 offering B.E. program in Information Technology with an intake of 60. Department also offers Hons. specialization in AI&ML. The department aims at imparting quality education in the realm of IT, software engineering and domain specific application with an objective to enhance and expand the knowledge and skill sets of target students, so that they can contribute in the betterment of Society at large. The main **objectives** of department are to provide Design and develop software products, Real time work experience and Employment. The department has well equipped **laboratories** like programming for problem solving using C / Python, data structures, database management system, programming with objects, Linux internals, big data and business analytics, case tools, web technologies, cloud application development, data warehousing and data mining, advanced python programming and object oriented modeling, etc. Department also has a **SAP Center of Excellence** and advance computing laboratories to cater advance technologies like AI, AR/VR, IoT, Data Science, Blockchain Technology etc.

### **Vision**

To empower IT graduates with cutting-edge knowledge and skills, enabling them to excel in a dynamic, technology-driven world, and contribute positively to society’s ever-evolving digital landscape.

### **Mission**

- To provide IT graduates with a world-class education that combines theoretical excellence with practical proficiency.
- To bridge the gap between academia and industry, facilitating internships, real-world projects, and networking opportunities for IT graduates
- To promote lifelong learning among IT graduates, enabling them to stay at the forefront of technological advancements throughout their careers.

### **Programme Educational Objectives(PEO)**

- The graduates will be able to interact with their peers in industries and society as an engineering professionals, entrepreneurs and leaders to set up technical ambience in the society
- The graduates will be able to reveal professionalism, ethical attitude, strong communication skills and maintain good team work spirit in their profession.
- The graduate will be able to utilize their skills with strong foundation to prepare them for higher learning and research activity.
- The graduate will be able to build up an ability to analyse the requirements, comprehend professional specifications, design and provide engineering solutions.

### **Programme Specific Outcomes(PSO)**

- Analyse, design and develop algorithmic solutions to any real life problems.
- To provide solutions for complex engineering problems by understanding the computer science concepts.
- To demonstrate the use of knowledge and ability to write programs and integrate them with software/hardware products in the domain of database, networking, data analytics, mobile communication technologies and web technologies.

### **MOU Signed**

- Department has signed M.O. U. with IBM Skillsbuild to provide 10000+ free IBM courses and free IBM Cloud Access and Training.
- Department has signed M.O. U. with SAP INDIA support for Excellence(COE) to give training on various SAP courses.
- Department has signed M.O. U. with Tata Strive SPOKE MODEL to give training in different various Emerging Technologies.
- Department has signed M.O. U. with LoftySoft AB (Sweden), Getwings Technologies Pvt. Ltd (Ahmedabad) for setting up “IoT Lab” and Centre of excellence in future.
- Department has signed M.O. U. with D-Link for D-Link Academy – An authorized Training Centre for D-Link certified Network Engineers.
- Department has been nominated as “Resource Centre” for Spoken Tutorial by IIT, Bombay, A project founded by MHRD, Government of Maharashtra.

## **Head Of Department**

# Prof. Vivek C. Joshi

Ph.D. (Pursuing), M.Tech. (COM. & SCI. ENGG), B. E. (Information Technology)

R. N. G. Patel Institute of Technology (RNGPIT),

At: Isroli, Po. Afwa, Tal. Bardoli, Dist. Surat -394620

E-mail: vcjoshi@rngpit.ac.in

MO. 9510922936

Prof. Vivek Joshi is a highly respected member of the faculty at RNGPIT’s Information Technology Department, with over 13 years of experience in academia and administration. With a Master’s degree in Computer Science & Engineering from VTU, Karnataka, and a Bachelor’s degree in Information Technology from the University of Pune, Prof. Vivek has been recognized for outstanding academic performance, earning a prestigious Gold Medal during his Master’s graduation. Currently, he is pursuing a Ph.D. in Artificial Intelligence at Gujarat Technological University, and their research focuses on developing non-invasive solutions for cancer detection using Deep Learning techniques. Dr. Vivek’s expertise encompasses a wide range of areas, including Data Science, Artificial Intelligence, Machine Learning, and Deep Learning. Prof. Vivek is not only involved in research but also plays a crucial role as the Coordinator for the Student Startup and Innovation Policy Cell, Government of Gujarat. In this capacity, he provides invaluable mentorship and guidance to students, assisting them in navigating the complexities of the Proof of Concepts, Prototypes, Intellectual Property Rights, and Startup ventures. Prof. Vivek has a wealth of practical experience, having filed and granted over ten industrial designs and patents, including an international patent, which underscores his commitment to fostering an environment of creativity and entrepreneurship within the academic community. Through his leadership, mentorship, and research efforts, Vivek continues to make significant contributions to both academia and industry, inspiring the next generation of innovators and thought leaders.

Information Technology (IT) department has established SAP sponsored “Center of Excellence(CoE) on Emerging Technologies” with Edunet Foundation. Industry experts are giving training on various SAP Course’s. After completion of course students are getting internship and placement opportunities.

Under this Centre of Excellence, IT students will get opportunity to be familiar with SAP Open Learning Platform and get Certification in the courses that are most relevant for the Engineering students considering the current skill trends required in the job market.

Courses Offered:

- SAP certified citizen developer associate, getting started with UX, SAP business technology platform, SAP Analytics cloud, ABAP programming, Foundations of SAP Commerce Cloud and SAP S/4HANA Cloud Integration.
- Industry experts will provide training on industry relevant topics like IOT, Artificial Intelligence, Machine Learning, Deep Learning, Computer Vision, High Performance Computing and Capstone Projects.

Objective/Outcome:

- To impart practical skills of various SAP modules and get industry ready SAP certified professionals.
- To provide training and certification on emerging technologies from industry experts.
- To provide placement support to the certified students.

Till date total 120 students of **Information Technology** & Engineering department are pursuing training and 78 students are already trained and certified.

1. The department’s four faculty was gold medalists of own times.
2. Department is offering GTU approved Specialization/Minor course on Artificial intelligence and machine learning since year 2020-2021
3. Department has established the SAP Sponsored “Center of Excellence (CoE) on Emerging Technologies” with Edunet Foundation.Industry experts are giving training on various SAP courses. After completion of course students are getting internship and placement opportunities.
4. Department has established IBM Skillsbuild Virtual CoE through Edunet foundation to impart practical skills in students to meet the challenging needs of industry, academia and society in the world of computer science and engineering.
5. The Department offers 10000+ free IBM courses under the IBM Skillsbuild Program. Students get Free IBM Cloud Access and Training by industry experts. Students can learn emerging technologies like Artificial Intelligence, Blockchain, Cloud Computing, Internet of Things, Cyber Security along with job Readiness and professional skills.
6. The Department has established SPOKE Model with TATA STRIVE for students training on various Emerging Technologies like cyber Security, UI UX Design, Full stack Development etc. After Completion of training students get benefited with placement opportunities by TATA STRIVE.
7. The Department has signed M.O. U. With Escrow Infotech, Agami Tech Pvt, Casepoint.Pvt., Siliconbrix Systems Pvt., Beetonz Infotech,Solwin Infotech, Coderkube Technologies, Toshal Infotech, Evolution Info Secure,Omtec Web for Skill Development, Outcome based Trainings, Internships, Placements and Related Services.
8. Two faculties of the department are pursuing their Ph.D. study under GTU.
9. Department currently has 16 MoU with the various Industries.
10. More than 11 patents have been published by faculties and students of the department.
11. Department is running courses viz. Domestic Data Entry Operator and Junior Software Developer under PMKVY which is a skill development initiative scheme of the Government of India for recognition and standardization of skills.

### ✅ Accreditation

- **Approved by**: National Board of Accreditation (NBA)

### **ABOUT DEPARTMENT**

- Welcome to our Civil Engineering Department, where passion drives purpose! Established in 2010, we are more than educators; we are architects of dreams and builders of careers. Our mission is clear: to create strong connections with the construction industry, academia, and research fields, fostering a holistic approach to civil engineering education and real-world applications. At our core, we thrive on active engagement in fundamental and applied research, contributing significantly to our field’s knowledge base. Our experienced faculty, experts in areas like Concrete Technology and Structural Engineering, bridge the gap between theory and practice, ensuring our students are well-prepared for the dynamic world of civil engineering. We believe in learning by doing, organizing industrial visits, expert lectures, and seminars that immerse students in real-world situations and cutting-edge technology. Our students grasp practical industry demands, giving them a competitive edge. Since 2020, our “Civil Material Testing Laboratory (CMTL)” has been a symbol of quality assurance, accredited by NABL and approved by GERI-Vadodara. We ensure precise testing, serving government departments and private agencies with dedication and affordability. In summary, our department nurtures well-rounded civil engineers. With dedicated faculty guidance and a focus on research and practical applications, our students gain technical expertise, resilience, adaptability, and problem-solving skills essential for success in the evolving world of civil engineering. Join us, and let’s build a future where challenges are opportunities and dreams are foundations!

### **Vision**

To establish a holistic teaching-learning ecosystem with a vision to create competent civil engineers and entrepreneur for the development of society and the nation.

### **Mission**

- To achieve quality in education through creating collaborative environment.
- To stimulate students for effective and efficient learning.
- To create civil engineering professionals with ability to apply knowledge professionally to reach their highest potential.
- To develop creative, dynamic technocrats and entrepreneurs.

### **Programme Educational Objectives(PEO)**

**An Experienced Civil Engineer of program:**

- Shall be able to apply fundamental technical knowledge and skills to find creative solutions to technological challenges and problems in various areas of civil engineering.
- Shall be able to analyse, design and use skills in order to formulate and solve civil engineering problems.
- Shall be able to practice civil engineering in a responsible professional and ethical manner and implement eco-friendly sustainable technologies for the benefits of industries and society.
- Shall be able to engage in research and development in civil engineering and allied branches of it.

### **Programme Specific Outcomes(PSO)**

**An Experienced Civil Engineer of program:**

- Shall be competent in all civil-related execution work by offering sustainable solutions to civil engineering problems both industry and society face.
- Shall be able to analyse and use advance technology for better performance and quality control of civil projects by workmanship and material.

### **Laboratory Certifications: Civil Material Testing Laboratory (CMTL)**

The laboratory at R.N.G. Patel Institute of Technology (RNGPIT) holds two major certifications:

- **NABL Accreditation (ISO/IEC 17025:2017):**
    - **Body:** National Accreditation Board for Testing and Calibration Laboratories (NABL).
    - **Certificate Number:** TC-9146.
    - **Field:** Testing.
    - **Validity:** Issued 08/12/2020; valid until 07/12/2022.
- **Quality Management (ISO 9001:2015):**
    - **Body:** ROHS Certification Pvt. Ltd..
    - **Scope:** Civil material testing and consultancy for building and road construction, including cement, aggregate, concrete, NDT of structures, steel, soil, bricks, and bitumen.
    - **Validity:** Valid from 01/02/2021 to 31/01/2024.

---

### **Vayumitra Skill Development Programme (VSDP)**

This is a flagship program of the **Ministry of New and Renewable Energy (MNRE)**, Government of India, implemented by the **National Institute of Wind Energy (NIWE)**.

- **Course Details:**
    - **Title:** Site Surveyor—Wind Power Plant.
    - **Venue:** R.N.G. Patel Institute of Technology, Bardoli (Authorized Training Partner).
    - **Duration:** 120 training hours (example session: Jan 23 to Feb 23, 2023).
    - **Fees:** No registration fees.
- **Eligibility & Participation:**
    - **Qualifications:** B.E. / B.Tech. in Electrical, Mechanical, Civil, Electronics and Communication, or Electrical and Electronics.
    - **Capacity:** Limited to 30 seats per batch on a first-come, first-served basis.
- **Learning Outcomes:**
    - Conducting site surveys for wind power plants.
    - Performing health and safety practices.
    - Carrying out site inspections and assessments.
    - Checking site access and grid/substation availability.
- **Partners:** Associate Partner M/s UGES PowerMax Pvt. Ltd., Vadodara.

### ✅ Accreditation

- **Approved by**: National Board of Accreditation (NBA)

**ABOUT DEPARTMENT**

“Education is not received, It is achieved” With this motto Electrical Engineering Department was established in year 2010 with the vision to convey practical knowledge in coordination with various Electrical industries and power sector companies. The department is persistently committed to enhancing technical knowledge along with the ethical values of the students to make them ready for the competitive world. With highly qualified & experienced faculties department has consistently tried to update itself for the constantly changing industrial needs, skills and challenges emerging out of new research also ensuring the best academic results from the students. The department is well equipped with 18 laboratories namely Elements of Electrical Engineering, Electrical & Electronics Workshop, Electrical Circuit Analysis, Electrical Machines (DC Machines & Transformer, AC Machines), Analog Electronics and Digital Electronics, Power System Protection, Control System Theory, Power Electronics, Electronic Measurement and Measuring Instruments, High Voltage Engineering, Elements of Electrical Design & Engineering Machine Design, Microprocessor and Microcontroller, Advanced Power Electronics, Advanced Power System, Interconnected Power System, Industrial Instrumentation, Computer Lab, Project Lab. All the laboratories are well equipped with the latest instruments & equipment which are updated on a regular basis which leads students to realize theory aspects intensely.

### **Vision**

To establish a holistic teaching-learning ecosystem with a vision to create competent Electrical technocrats with ethical values contributing to the sustainable growth of society and the nation.

### **Mission**

- **M1:** To ensure effective and efficient teaching methods with technical excellence for sound fundamentals.
- **M2:** To impart technical skills through effective practical work and Vocational Training.
- **M3:** To use modern tools and technology for better correlation of theory with practices.
- **M4:** To develop creative, dynamic technocrats and entrepreneurs.

### **Program Outcomes**

- **PO1 Engineering knowledge:** Apply the knowledge of mathematics, science, engineering fundamentals, and an engineering specialization to the solution of complex engineering problems.
- **PO2 Problem analysis:** Identify, formulate, research literature, and analyze complex engineering problems reaching substantiated conclusions using first principles of mathematics, natural sciences, and engineering sciences.
- **PO3 Design/development of solutions:** Design solutions for complex engineering problems and design system components or processes that meet the specified needs with appropriate consideration for public health and safety, and cultural, societal, and environmental considerations.
- **PO4 Conduct investigations of complex problems:** Use research-based knowledge and research methods including design of experiments, analysis and interpretation of data, and synthesis of the information to provide valid conclusions.
- **PO5 Modern tool usage:** Create, select, and apply appropriate techniques, resources, and modern engineering and IT tools including prediction and modelling to complex engineering activities with an understanding of the limitations.
- **PO6 The engineer and society:** Apply reasoning informed by the contextual knowledge to assess societal, health, safety, legal, and cultural issues and the consequent responsibilities relevant to the professional engineering practice.
- **PO7 Ethics**: Apply ethical principles and commit to professional ethics and responsibilities and norms of the engineering practice.
- **PO8 Individual and team work**: Function effectively as an individual, and as a member or leader in diverse teams, and in multidisciplinary settings.
- **PO9 Communication:** Communicate effectively on complex engineering activities with the engineering community and with society at large, such as being able to comprehend and write effective reports and design documentation, make effective presentations, and give and receive clear instructions.
- **PO10 Project management and finance:** Demonstrate knowledge and understanding of the engineering and management principles and apply these to one’s own work, as a member and leader in a team, to manage projects and in multidisciplinary environments.
- **PO11 Life-long learning:** Recognize the need for, and have the preparation and ability to engage in independent and life-long learning in the broadest context of technological change.

### **Program Specific Outcomes (PSO)**

Graduate Electrical Engineer will be able to

- **PSO1:** Identify, formulate and solve realistic electrical problems in operation of Electrical machines, and equipment used in power generation, transmission and utilization.
- **PSO2:** Proficient in assessing clean energy options, and energy-efficient solutions to promote sustainable development.

### **Program Educational Objectives(PEO)**

- **PEO1:** Graduates will apply basic and advanced knowledge to solve complex electrical problems and usage of modern tools in the field of electrical engineering along with inter-disciplinary fields.
- **PEO2:** Graduates will apply academic knowledge to formulate and address real-world phenomena contributing to innovative solutions, and upholding professional values.
- **PEO3:** Graduates will master skill-based knowledge to prepare them for higher education competitive exams and an entrepreneurial mind-set for successful career paths in electrical engineering.
- **PEO4:** Graduates will exhibit effective communication skills, teamwork and leadership qualities for society’s betterment and lifelong learning.

**3. Civil Material Testing Laboratory (CMTL) Certifications**
The lab is a certified facility managed by the Sardar Vallabhbhai Patel Education Society.
• **NABL Accreditation (ISO/IEC 17025:2017):** Recognized for the "General Requirements for the Competence of Testing & Calibration Laboratories".
• **ISO 9001:2015 Registration:** Certified for quality management in civil material testing and consultancy for buildings and roads.
• **Scope of Testing:** Cement, aggregate, fresh and hardened concrete, NDT of structures, steel, soil, bricks, and bitumen.
**4. Vayumitra Skill Development Programme (VSDP)**
A free training initiative sponsored by the **Ministry of New and Renewable Energy (MNRE)**.
• **Course:** Site Surveyor—Wind Power Plant (120 training hours).
• **Eligibility:** B.E./B.Tech graduates in Electrical, Mechanical, Civil, Electronics & Communication, or Control & Instrumentation.
• **Participation:** Limited to 30 seats per batch on a first-come, first-served basis.

**Department Achievements**

1. **Electrical Engineering Department Achieves NBA Accreditation in 2024!**
    
    Our **Electrical Engineering Department** proudly received **NBA Accreditation in 2024**, securing **a score above 650**. This recognition reflects our **commitment to academic excellence, quality education, and industry standards**.
    
2. Electrical Engineering department at R N G Patel Institute, Bardoli, has achieved outstanding results in the GTU examinations. In the Summer 2023 Examination, we secured the 1st rank in Semester 8, the 3rd rank in Semester 2, and the 6th rank in Semester 4.
3. Electrical Engineering department at R.N.G. Patel Institute of Technology (RNGPIT) Bardoli has signed MOU with “SSM Infotech Surat” for Skill development and Outcome based Training of students. This MOU will give internship and placement opportunities to Electrical Engineering student.
4. Electrical Engineering department at R N G Patel Institute, Bardoli, in the Winter 2022 examinations. Our department secured the 5th rank in Semester 3 and the 8th rank in Semester 1.
5. Electrical Engineering department at R N G Patel Institute, Bardoli, in the GTU Summer 2021 examinations. Achieving the 1st rank in Semesters 8, 6, and 2 reflects the continuous dedication to excellence demonstrated by our students and faculty.

# **B.Voc. in Software Development:
—>**

### **ABOUT DEPARTMENT**

B.Voc. – Bachelor of Vocation in Software Development is responsible for designing, developing, testing and software engineer, evaluating the software that make our computers work. This involves designing and coding of new programs and applications to meet the needs of a business or an individual. The department is highly reputed in academic and industry for its integrity, faculties, infrastructure, placement, industry linkage and applications development. Environment of the department is professional and friendly, students can access resources any time, and faculties are available round the clock. The department proudly upholds excellent training records and emerging career opportunities for students. The Software Development Department has been established with an aim to prepare skilful software developers in the field of computers. The department has developed a HPVTC computer centre with 100 Nos of computers connected in LAN with 50Mbps internet connectivity and latest licenced applications and system softwares.

### **VISION:**

To empower IT graduates with cutting-edge knowledge and skills, enabling them to excel in a dynamic, technology-driven world, and contribute positively to society’s ever-evolving digital landscape.

### **MISSION:**

- To provide IT graduates with a world-class education that combines theoretical excellence with practical proficiency.
- To bridge the gap between academia and industry, facilitating internships, real-world projects, and networking opportunities for IT graduates.
- To promote lifelong learning among IT graduates, enabling them to stay at the forefront of technological advancements throughout their careers.

### **MOU Signed:**

- Department has signed M.O. U. with IBM Skillsbuild to provide 10000+ free IBM courses and free IBM Cloud Access and Training.
- Department has signed M.O. U. with SAP INDIA support for Excellence(COE) to give training on various SAP courses.
- Department has signed M.O. U. with Tata Strive SPOKE MODEL to give training in different various Emerging Technologies.
- Department has signed M.O. U. with LoftySoft AB (Sweden), Getwings Technologies Pvt. Ltd (Ahmedabad) for setting up “IoT Lab” and Centre of excellence in future.
- Department has signed M.O. U. with D-Link for D-Link Academy – An authorized Training Centre for D-Link certified Network Engineers.
- Department has been nominated as “Resource Centre” for Spoken Tutorial by IIT, Bombay, A project founded by MHRD, Government of Maharashtra.

**R. N. G. Patel Institute of Technology (RNGPIT)**, in association with **Kingston University, London**, is offering a unique "Academic Progression Programme."

- **Course Structure:** A combined degree consisting of a **3-year B.Voc in Software Development** (studied in India) followed by a **1-year M.Sc** (studied in London).
- **M.Sc Specializations:** Students can choose Software Engineering, Data Science, or Information Systems.
- **Eligibility:** Open to 12th-grade students from **any stream** (Commerce, Arts, Vocation, or Science).
- **Key Benefit:** Graduates are eligible for a **two-year work permit visa** after completing the 1-year Master's program in London.

BVOC-SOFTWARE DEVELOPMENT OJT:

—>

 the **1200 hours of On-Job Training (OJT)** included in the curriculum. It breaks down the specific vocational focus for each of the six semesters:

**Table Details** 

- **Semester:** Semester-1 | **Name of OJT:** Database System Assistant
- **Semester:** Semester-2 | **Name of OJT:** Web Developer
- **Semester:** Semester-3 | **Name of OJT:** User Experince Designer

**Table Details** 

- **Semester:** Semester-4 | **Name of OJT:** Advanced Web Technology
- **Semester:** Semester-5 | **Name of OJT:** Android Programming
- **Semester:** Semester-6 | **Name of OJT:** Application Developer Web & Mobile

- **Year 1:** Focuses on backend basics (Database Systems) and frontend foundations (Web Development, UI/UX).
- **Year 2:** Moves to advanced web tech and Android programming.
- **Year 3:** Culminates in comprehensive Web & Mobile Application development.

**ABOUT DEPARTMENT**

R. N. G. Patel Institute of Technology (F.E.T. R.) has launched AICTE, New Delhi approved and Gujarat Technological University (GTU) affiliated three years graduation programme on skill development based higher education as part of college education, leading to Bachelor of Vocation (B. Voc.) Degree. The B.Voc. programme is focused on providing undergraduate studies which would also incorporate specific job roles along with broad based general education. This would enable the graduates completing B. Voc. to make a meaningful participation in accelerating India’ economy by gaining appropriate employment, becoming entrepreneurs and creating appropriate knowledge. All the students who have passed Std. 12th(Science / Commerce / Arts/ Vocational) are eligible for this course.

Our Institute has three B. Voc. Courses run at H. B. Punawala Vocational Training Center.

Our Institute has inaugurated B.Voc. Programme on 13th August 2018 in the presence of Prof. (Dr.) Shailesh D. Panchal,[Registrar, GTU], Mr. Atik Desai, [Vice President & CAO, L & T, Hazira], various industries, co-operative leaders and educationalist.

### 

### **Objectives:**

- To provide judicious mix of skills relating to a profession and appropriate content of General Education.
- To ensure that the students have adequate knowledge and skills, so that they are work ready at each exit point of the programme.
- To provide flexibility to the students by means of pre-defined entry and multiple exit points.
- To interchange NSQF within the undergraduate level of higher education in order to enhance employability of the graduates and meet industry requirements. Such graduates apart from meeting the needs of local and national industry are also expected to be equipped to become part of the global workforce.
- To provide vertical mobility to students coming out of 10+2 with vocational subjects.
- Skill knowledge Provide (SKP) gives On-job Training in each semester.

### **List of On -Job Training offered in each semester in B. Voc.**

| **COURSE NAME** | **INTAKE** | **DURATION** | **ELIGIBILITY** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| B. VOC. IN PRODUCTION TECHNOLOGY | 30 | 3 YEARS | 10+2 OR EQUIVALENT IN ANY STREAM |
| B. VOC. IN SOFTWARE DEVELOPMENT | 60 | 3 YEARS | 10+2 OR EQUIVALENT IN ANY STREAM |
| B. VOC. IN REFRIGERATION & AIR CONDITIONING | 30 | 3 YEARS | 10+2 OR EQUIVALENT IN ANY STREAM |

- To ensure that the students have adequate knowledge and skills, so that they are work ready at each exit point of the programme.
- To provide flexibility to the students by means of pre-defined entry and multiple exit points.
- To interchange NSQF within the undergraduate level of higher education in order to enhance employability of the graduates and meet industry requirements. Such graduates apart from meeting the needs of local and national industry are also expected to be equipped to become part of the global workforce.
- To provide vertical mobility to students coming out of 10+2 with vocational subjects.
- Skill knowledge Provide (SKP) gives On-job Training in each semester.

# Degree Offered :

## **B.Voc. in Solar and Renewable Energy**

### **ABOUT DEPARTMENT**

The **Department of Solar and Renewable Energy** at **R.N.G. Patel Institute of Technology (RNGPIT)** is dedicated to providing **industry-oriented education** in the field of **solar and renewable energy technologies**. Our **B.Voc. (Bachelor of Vocation) in Solar and Renewable Energy** program is designed to equip students with **practical skills and real-world experience** to meet the growing demands of the renewable energy sector.

With **state-of-the-art facilities**, strong **industry collaborations**, and an **application-based learning approach**, we ensure that students gain **hands-on experience** in solar and wind energy systems. The program is **approved by AICTE, affiliated with GTU**, and follows a **skill-based curriculum** with a focus on **on-job training (OJT)**.

### 

- **Comprehensive Skill-Based Curriculum:** A **full-time, three-year graduation course** integrating technical knowledge and practical training.
- **Extensive On-Job Training:** Over **1200+ hours of industry exposure** through hands-on projects.
- **Industry Partnerships:** Collaborations with leading renewable energy companies to enhance **job prospects and placements**.
- **Cutting-Edge Infrastructure:** Our **210 kW solar power plant** provides a **live learning platform**, fulfilling **40% of the campus’s energy needs**.
- **Highest University Rankings:** Our students consistently achieve **top rankings in university exams**.

img :

### **Institutional Information**

- **Name:** R.N.G. Patel Institute of Technology (RNGPIT).
- **Management:** Sardar Vallabhbhai Patel Education Society.
- **Credentials:** AICTE Approved, affiliated with GTU (Gujarat Technological University).
- **Specialized Center:** H.B. Punawala Vocational Training & Research Centre.

### **Course 1: B.Voc (Refrigeration & Air-Conditioning)**

- **Duration:** 3 Years (6 Semesters).
- **Eligibility:** Std. 12 (Science/Commerce/Vocation/Arts).
- **Key Features:** Full-time skill-based graduation, 1200+ hours On-Job Training (OJT), industry-oriented learning.
- **Curriculum Focus (Semester-wise OJT):**
    - **Sem 1:** Field Technician – Refrigeration
    - **Sem 2:** Field Technician – AC
    - **Sem 3:** Dehydration Technician
    - **Sem 4:** Cold Storage Technician
    - **Sem 5:** AC Specialist – Automobile
    - **Sem 6:** Assembly Operator
- **Placement (First Batch):** 100% placement at Purvi Engineering India Pvt. Ltd. (Surat) with packages up to **1.20 LPA**.
- **Job Scope:** Design, install, and maintain systems in supermarkets, offices, hospitals, and food plants.

### **Course 2: B.Voc (Solar and Renewable Energy)**

- **Infrastructure:** Campus includes a **210 kW** solar power plant using Bifacial Halfcut Monopark 660 W panels, fulfilling 40% of campus demand.
- **Curriculum Focus (Semester-wise OJT):**
    - **Sem 1:** Assistant Electrician
    - **Sem 2:** Solar PV Installer (Suryamitra)
    - **Sem 3:** Solar PV Module Manufacturing Technician
    - **Sem 4:** O&M Electrical & Instrumentation Technician – Wind Power Plant
    - **Sem 5:** Solar PV O&M Engineer
    - **Sem 6:** Rooftop Solar Grid Engineer

### **Industry Partnerships (MOUs)**

MOUs are signed with several Surat-based companies for training:

- Purvi Engineering India Pvt. Ltd.
- Raj Refrigeration System (Conducted 2020 batch OJT)
- Future HVAC System
- RENOM (Solar Partner)
- UGES (Solar Partner)
- ISHARE Student Chapter & Master Services (Bardoli)

### **On-Job Training (OJT) Offered in Each Semester**

- **Semester 1:** Assistant Electrician
- **Semester 2:** Solar PV Installer (Suryamitra)
- **Semester 3:** Solar PV Module Manufacturing Technician
- **Semester 4:** O&M Electrical & Instrumentation Technician (Wind Power Plant)
- **Semester 5:** Solar PV O&M Engineer
- **Semester 6:** Rooftop Solar Grid Engineer

### **Institutional Overview**

- **Name:** R.N.G. Patel Institute of Technology (RNGPIT).
- **Management:** Sardar Vallabhbhai Patel Education Society.
- **Credentials:** AICTE Approved and affiliated with **Gujarat Technological University (GTU)**.
- **Infrastructure:** Includes the **H.B. Punawala Vocational Training & Research Centre** and a **210 kW** bifacial solar power plant on campus.

### **2. B.Voc Programs (3-Year Full-Time Degree)**

Both courses require a **Std. 12 (Science/Commerce/Arts/Vocation)** pass for eligibility and offer **1200+ hours** of on-job training (OJT).

### **Specialization: Refrigeration & Air-Conditioning**

- **Capacity:** 25 sanctioned seats.
- **Fees:** Approximately **₹1.05 Lakhs to ₹1.35 Lakhs** for the total duration.
- **Training Curriculum (Semester-wise OJT):**
    - **SEM 1 & 2:** Field Technician (Refrigeration & AC).
    - **SEM 3 & 4:** Dehydration & Cold Storage Technician.
    - **SEM 5 & 6:** AC Specialist (Automobile) & Assembly Operator.
- **Placement (First Batch):** 100% placement at **Purvi Engineering India Pvt. Ltd.** with starting packages up to **1.20 LPA**.

### **Specialization: Solar and Renewable Energy**

- **Infrastructure:** Campus power plant fulfills **40% of demand** (avg. 900 units/day).
- **Training Curriculum (Semester-wise OJT):**
    - **SEM 1 & 2:** Assistant Electrician & Solar PV Installer (Suryamitra).
    - **SEM 3 & 4:** Module Manufacturing & Wind Power Plant O&M Technician.
    - **SEM 5 & 6:** Solar PV O&M Engineer & Rooftop Solar Grid Engineer.

### **3. Industry Partnerships (MOUs)**

The institute has formal agreements with several Surat-based companies for practical exposure:

- **HVAC:** Purvi Engineering India, Raj Refrigeration System, and Future HVAC System.
- **Renewable Energy:** RENOM and UGES.

### **3. B.Voc Program: Solar and Renewable Energy**

# SARDAR VALLABHBHAI PATEL EDUCATION SOCIETY

Sardar Vallabhbhai Patel Education Society (SVPES) is the public trust which comprises of persons who believe in giving the best education to the upcoming generation in such a way to serve the nation and society at their best. This society is established in the year 1996.

Under the leadership and visionary mind of Mr. R. S. Patel and his team, started a first polytechnic in this area in 1997 named N. G. Patel Polytechnic (NGPP). Since inception NGPP provides quality technical education and prepared thousands of technocrats for the industries.

In 2010 SVPES has started Degree engineering college named R. N. G. Patel Institute of Technology (RNGPIT) with an aim to achieve excellence in engineering and technology, ethical values and along with overall development of students.

## DETAILS ABOUT Integrated M.Sc. IT

## Integrated M.Sc. IT
ABOUT DEPARTMENT

Integrated M.SC. (IT), Course is a Full Time, 5 years Program. Its aims at grooming the software skills, enhancing the problem-solving capacity of students, and thus fulfilling the industry demands. The course is designed to meet up with the demands of qualified professionals in IT Industry. It is a computer science degree whose curriculum is largely based on the development of application software in diverse areas. The course describes an in-depth and extensive knowledge about computer applications. The course is intended to expose the students to various areas of computer applications with the objective of eliminating any gaps between industry and academia.

To emphasize more on practical aspects with current trends and technologies, the department has developed laboratories with 80 Nos. of computers connected in LAN with 500 Mbps internet connectivity and latest licensed applications & system software’s. 

Vision:
 

To establish a holistic teaching-learning ecosystem dedicated to cultivating dynamic IT professionals and entrepreneurs who drive societal, national, and global development.

Mission:
To ensure effective and efficient teaching-learning process for sound fundamentals.
To provide specialized training in cutting-edge IT technologies to develop technical expertise among students.
Equip graduates with the skills and mindset necessary to excel as employable IT professionals and innovative entrepreneurs.
To inculcate human values, creative problem solving & research capabilities, leadership abilities and ethics.
Programme Educational Objectives:
The graduates will be able to interact with their peers in industries and society as an engineering professionals, entrepreneurs and leaders to set up technical ambience in the society
The graduates will be able to reveal professionalism, ethical attitude, strong communication skills and maintain good team work spirit in their profession.
The graduate will be able to utilize their skills with strong foundation to prepare them for higher learning and research activity.
The graduate will be able to build up an ability to analyse the requirements, comprehend professional specifications, design and provide engineering solutions.
Programme Specific Outcomes(PSOs):
Analyse, design and develop algorithmic solutions to any real life problems.
To provide solutions for complex engineering problems by understanding the computer science concepts.
To demonstrate the use of knowledge and ability to write programs and integrate them with software/hardware products in the domain of database, networking, data analytics, mobile communication technologies and web technologies.
MOU Outcomes
Department has signed M.O. U. with IBM Skillsbuild to provide 10000+ free IBM courses and free IBM Cloud Access and Training. 
Department has signed M.O. U. with Tata Strive SPOKE MODEL to give training in different various Emerging Technologies.  
Department has been nominated as “Resource Centre” for Spoken Tutorial by IIT, Bombay, A project founded by MHRD, Government of Maharashtra.

At Integrated M. Sc. (I.T.) , we are committed to excellence in Information Technology. Our mission is to foster innovation, critical thinking, and leadership among our students. Through a blend of academic rigor, research opportunities, and industry collaborations, we strive to equip our students with the knowledge and skills needed to excel in today’s dynamic world.

Our faculty members are dedicated experts, passionate about mentoring and guiding individuals to achieve their full potential. We emphasize not only theoretical learning but also practical exposure, ensuring that our graduates are well-prepared for real-world challenges.

We invite you to explore our department, engage with our initiatives, and be part of a vibrant learning and professional community.

## M. Sc. IT Brochures:
# Integrated M.Sc. (Information Technology) Department  
### (B.Sc. I.T. + M.Sc. I.T.)

---

## Eligibility
Candidates who have passed:

- **HSC / 12th Standard**
- **Science Stream**
- **Arts / Commerce / Vocational**
- **10+2 Equivalent Diploma**

---

## Department Profile

The **Integrated M.Sc. (IT)** course is a **Full-Time 5-year program**.

- Aims to groom students into **software developers, designers, and administrators**.
- Designed to meet the **demand of professionals in the IT industry**.
- Emphasizes **domain-specific applications and practical exposure**.

---

## Vision

To establish a **holistic teaching–learning ecosystem** with a vision to prepare **dynamic IT professionals and entrepreneurs** for the development of society and the nation.

---

## Mission

- Ensure **effective and efficient teaching-learning processes** for strong fundamentals.
- Provide **technical skills through effective specialized training** using modern IT technologies.
- Prepare **employable IT professionals and entrepreneurs**.
- Encourage **human values, creative problem solving, research capabilities, leadership qualities, and ethics**.

---

## AMCAT – AI Based Computer Adaptive Test

The **Integrated M.Sc. (IT) Department** of **R.N.G. Patel Institute of Technology (RNGPIT), Bardoli** has established **SHL Pvt. Ltd., Gurgaon** as a support partner.

Benefits include:

- **Placement-oriented skill assessment programs**
- **Enhancement of technical and employability skills**
- Helps recruiters assess **domain skills** and find **suitable candidates for job roles**

---

## Glimpse of Department Activities

- AMCAT Exam
- Student Presentation on Brief IT Topics
- ITSM Meetings
- Highly Configured Laboratories
- Class Toppers Recognition
